TL;DR: 提出了一种结合Feynman路径求和与稳定子框架仿真的Clifford+T量子电路精确振幅模拟器，通过递归多级二分、自动回退和成本模型驱动分区选择，在结构化基准测试中实现了最高17,645倍的加速，并揭示了切割计数最小化作为代理指标的不可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稳定子的Schrödinger-Feynman方法存在三个局限：仅使用单级二分、无内存自适应回退机制、依赖切割计数最小化这一不可靠的启发式方法。切割计数减少可能因T门密度不均衡反而增加运行时间，亟需更精确的成本模型。

Method: 1) 递归多级二分构建二叉树，将平衡量子比特二分上的Feynman路径求和与稳定子框架仿真结合；2) 当叶节点稳定子帧超出内存上限时自动回退到稠密态矢量仿真；3) 用成本模型替代切割计数最小化启发式，该模型用2^w替换稠密2^n边界，显式建模每振幅读出成本，并修复了叶仿真器中路径末端振幅提取的二次渐近低效问题（替换为O(F*s*n)单振幅内积）。

Result: 在结构化n=16分层基准测试中，递归模拟器比单层稳定子帧仿真快92倍至17,645倍；相同切割下比稠密半态矢量基线快79倍；比生产级态矢量模拟器快最多47.9倍（中位数~5倍）。在随机Clifford+T对抗性电路中，稠密态矢量在交叉切割门数接近n/2时胜出——该交叉点由成本模型识别。交叉切割Feynman求和具有天然并行性，通信开销恒定。

Conclusion: 切割计数最小化是实践中不可靠的代理指标；所提出的成本模型能准确预测性能瓶颈并指导分区选择；递归多级结构配合自适应回退机制可显著加速结构化Clifford+T电路模拟；交叉切割Feynman求和的并行优势优于矩阵乘积态稳定子张量方法。

Abstract: We present an exact amplitude simulator for Clifford+T quantum circuits that combines a Feynman path sum across a balanced qubit bipartition with stabilizer-frame simulation on each half. The construction extends prior stabilizer-based Schrödinger-Feynman methods in three directions: recursive multilevel bipartition into a binary tree, automatic fallback to dense state-vector simulation when a leaf's stabilizer frame would exceed its memory ceiling, and a cost-model-driven partition selector that replaces the standard cut-count minimization heuristic.
  We show cut-count minimization is an unreliable proxy in practice: a globally cleaner partition can reduce cross-cut count yet increase wall-clock time, because it imbalances T-gate density across halves and inflates per-half stabilizer-frame size. Our cost model substitutes the stabilizer-frame bound 2w for the dense 2n ceiling per side and explicitly models per-amplitude readout cost; isolating that term uncovered a quadratic-asymptotic inefficiency in the leaf simulator's end-of-path amplitude extraction, fixed by replacing it with an existing O(F * s * n) single-amplitude inner product.
  On a structured hierarchical n=16 benchmark the recursive simulator beats monolithic stabilizer-frame simulation by 92x to 17,645x, wins by 79x per path against a dense half-state-vector baseline under an identical cut, and beats a production state-vector simulator end to end by up to 47.9x (median ~5x). On adversarial random Clifford+T circuits the dense state vector wins past a crossover near n/2 cross-cut gates -- the regime the cost model identifies. The dominant cost, the cross-cut Feynman sum, is embarrassingly parallel with constant inter-worker communication, unlike recent matrix-product-state stabilizer-tensor methods whose inner contraction loop is sequential.

</details>


### [95] [Native CCZ Gate with Fluxonium Qubits and a Microwave-Driven Coupler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27094)
*Grigoriy S. Mazhorin,Tatyana A. Chudakova,Alena S. Kazmina,Nikolai G. Berezkin,Arina V. Zotova,Artyom M. Polyanskiy,Nikolay N. Abramov,Mikhail A. Tarkhov,Alexander M. Mumlyakov,Igor V. Trofimov,Elizaveta A. Krivko,Nikita Yu. Rudenko,Maxim V. Chichkov,Vladimir I. Chichkov,Ilya A. Simakov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在基于fluxonium量子比特和微波驱动transmon耦合器的三量子比特处理器单元中，实验实现了一个65纳秒的原生受控-受控-相位门（等价于Toffoli门），保真度达99.39(5)%，仅需单一控制脉冲和简单校准，且可扩展至二维布局，证明了原生多量子比特门可作为可扩展超导量子处理器的高效硬件原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生多量子比特门有望减少分解为单双量子比特操作的开销，但其是否能在可扩展架构中同时提供高保真度、简单控制以及对寄生相互作用的鲁棒性尚不清楚，本文旨在实验验证这一可行性。

Method: 在fluxonium量子比特与微波驱动transmon耦合器构成的三量子比特处理器单元中，通过单一控制脉冲实现65纳秒的原生CCZ门，采用简单校准程序获得 coherence-limited 性能，并自然扩展至二维可扩展布局。

Result: 实现的原生CCZ门保真度为99.39(5)%，若通过传统分解实现则需约99.94%的CZ保真度；门操作仅需单一控制脉冲，且可扩展至低寄生相互作用的二维布局。

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了原生多量子比特门作为可扩展超导量子处理器中硬件高效原语的可行性。

Abstract: Native multi-qubit gates could reduce the overhead associated with decompositions into single- and two-qubit operations, but whether they can simultaneously provide high fidelity, simple control and robustness against parasitic interactions in scalable architectures remains unclear. Here we experimentally realize a 65-ns native controlled-controlled-phase operation, locally equivalent to the Toffoli gate, with a fidelity of 99.39(5)% in a three-qubit processor unit based on fluxonium qubits coupled via a microwave-driven transmon coupler. The implemented operation would require CZ fidelities of approximately 99.94% if realized through a conventional decomposition. The gate is implemented with a single control pulse, that relies on a simple calibration procedure yielding coherence-limited performance. This processor unit naturally extends to scalable two-dimensional layouts with low parasitic interactions. Altogether, these results establish native multi-qubit gates as a viable hardware-efficient primitive for scalable superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [96] [Effective Hamiltonians for Predictive Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27111)
*Razvan Stanescu,Hugo Ribeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超导transmon量子比特中，基于Duffing近似和对角化transmon模型两种不同哈密顿量模型对单量子比特门保真度的影响，发现模型差异（能谱和驱动算符表示）会导致动力学演化的不同，进而影响纠偏脉冲的有效性，并指出忽略相关泄漏通道会限制控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真量子控制依赖于精确的驱动动力学模型。本文旨在检验超导transmon量子比特中，标准Duffing近似与对角化transmon模型两种不同哈密顿量模型对单量子比特门控制的影响，探究模型依赖性的具体表现及其对纠偏脉冲有效性的影响。

Method: 对比两种模型（Duffing近似和对角化transmon模型）下的控制脉冲，使用相同的纠偏脉冲结构，分别在模型内和跨模型验证纠偏效果；分析能谱和驱动算符表征的差异；引入AC Stark相位累积作为动力学位移依赖性的诊断指标；提出扩展纠偏框架以包含相关泄漏通道和高阶误差通道。

Result: Duffing近似导出的纠偏场在该模型内能显著降低门误差，但迁移到对角化transmon模型时效果减弱；在快门区域甚至无法改善未纠偏的对角化transmon基线。模型间的微小差异（能谱和驱动算符表征）在驱动演化过程中会累积放大，导致不同的误差生成器和纠偏脉冲。AC Stark相位的不匹配是模型依赖性的一个指示性诊断。忽略相关泄漏通道或高阶误差通道会导致过度受限的纠偏策略。

Conclusion: 模型哈密顿量的选择直接影响控制框架的设计：忽略相关泄漏通道或高阶误差通道会限制纠偏策略的有效性。包含这些通道的扩展纠偏框架能在相同物理控制资源下提升门性能。因此，精确的模型选择和完整的误差通道考虑对于实现高保真量子门控制至关重要。

Abstract: High-fidelity quantum control relies on accurate models of driven dynamics. We examine this re- quirement for single-qubit gates in superconducting transmons by comparing control pulses derived from the standard Duffing approximation and from a Hamiltonian constructed by diagonalizing the transmon eigenbasis. Using the same correction-pulse construction for both models, we show that correction fields derived from the Duffing approximation can substantially reduce the gate error pre- dicted by that model while remaining less effective when combined with an independently calibrated baseline pulse in the diagonalized-transmon model. In the fast-gate regime, such transferred correc- tions can even fail to improve over the uncorrected diagonalized-transmon baseline. We show that small model-dependent differences in both the energy spectrum and the representation of the drive operator can compound during driven evolution, resulting in different predicted error generators and correction pulses. A mismatch in the accumulated AC Stark phase provides one illustrative di- agnostic of this dynamical model dependence. We further demonstrate that the model Hamiltonian informs the choice of control framework: Omitting relevant leakage pathways or higher-order error channels can lead to an overly restricted correction strategy. Including these channels motivates an extended correction framework that improves the gate performance using the same physical control resources.

</details>


### [97] [Ky Fan majorization for binary tensor products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27116)
*Michael M. Wolf,Yuanheng Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了二元张量积矩阵之和的奇异值的Ky Fan型优超关系的简短证明，将Alhejji的结果从两项和推广到任意和，从正矩阵推广到任意矩阵，并应用于完全正映射与其Kraus算子奇异值之间的优超关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Alhejji, arXiv:2410.18254）仅证明了两个二元张量积之和的奇异值优超关系，且局限于正矩阵情形。本文旨在将该结果推广到任意多个求和项以及任意矩阵（不要求正性），从而建立更一般的奇异值优超框架。

Method: 采用Ky Fan型优超关系的证明技巧，通过对二元张量积矩阵之和的奇异值进行不等式推导，给出简洁的证明。证明不依赖于矩阵的正性假设，直接处理一般矩阵情形。

Result: 成功将Alhejji的结果从两项和推广到任意有限项和，并将适用矩阵范围从正矩阵扩展到任意矩阵。同时证明了完全正映射的奇异值与其Kraus算子奇异值之间的优超关系。

Conclusion: 本文建立的推广型优超关系为量子信息论中完全正映射的奇异值分析提供了新工具，表明Kraus算子的奇异值在优超意义上控制着对应完全正映射的奇异值。

Abstract: We provide a short proof of a Ky Fan-type majorization relation for the singular values of a sum of binary tensor products of matrices. This generalizes Alhejji's result (arXiv:2410.18254) from two summands to arbitrary sums and from positive matrices to arbitrary matrices. As an application, we show a majorization relation between the singular values of a completely positive map and those of its Kraus operators.

</details>


### [98] [The Keyl-Werner algorithm is not optimal for spectrum estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27117)
*Angelos Pelecanos,Jack Spilecki,Ewin Tang,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的量子态谱估计算法，仅需 n = O(d²·(log log d / log d)²) 份拷贝就能以常数总变分距离误差估计出量子态的特征值，优于 Keyl-Werner 算法所需的 Θ(d²) 份拷贝，解决了 Keyl 和 Werner 在 2001 年提出的问题，并反驳了 Wright 在 2016 年的猜想。核心技术创新是一种"相对误差"层析成像保证，该技术还衍生出主成分分析和 χ²-散度层析成像的改进算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，量子态谱估计（特征值学习）的最优样本复杂度一直悬而未决。Keyl-Werner 算法（2001年）需要 Θ(d²) 份拷贝，而 Wright 在 2016 年猜想这个下界是紧的。本文旨在证明通过更精细的分析可以突破这个下界，用更少的拷贝数实现同样准确的谱估计。

Method: 提出了一种新的"相对误差"层析成像保证：对于任意方向 |w⟩，层析误差与 ⟨w|ρ|w⟩ 成比例，即所有方向同时满足相对误差界。基于这一更强的保证，构造了改进的谱估计算法，样本复杂度为 n = O(d²·(log log d / log d)²)。

Result: 1) 谱估计的样本复杂度从 Θ(d²) 降低到 O(d²·(log log d / log d)²)；2) 解决了 Keyl-Werner 在 2001 年提出的开放问题；3) 反驳了 Wright 在 2016 年的猜想；4) 作为推论，获得了 Bures 距离下主成分分析和 χ²-散度下层析成像的改进算法。

Conclusion: 量子态谱估计的样本复杂度可以低于完全层析成像所需的 Θ(d²)，突破了过去认为的紧下界。相对误差层析成像这一工具不仅改进了谱估计，还带来了主成分分析和层析成像等下游任务的性能提升，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We give an algorithm which, given $n = O(d^2 \cdot (\log\log(d)/\log(d))^2)$ copies of $ρ$, estimates the eigenvalues of $ρ$ to constant error in total variation distance. Thus, we can learn the eigenvalues of a quantum state with fewer copies than the $Θ(d^2)$ needed to run full state tomography. This is the first improvement to spectrum estimation over the influential Keyl-Werner algorithm, which uses $n = Θ(d^2)$ copies, thereby resolving a question raised by Keyl and Werner in 2001 and refuting a 2016 conjecture of Wright.
  Our main technical tool is a new tomography guarantee, where the error of tomography in a particular direction $|w\rangle$ scales with $\langle w | ρ|w\rangle$ for all directions simultaneously. From this stronger "relative-error" bound, we recover better algorithms for principal component analysis in Bures distance and tomography in $χ^2$-divergence as corollaries.

</details>


### [99] [Hardware-efficient erasure-error detection with an integer fluxonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27123)
*Junyoung An,Helin Zhang,Jeffrey M. Gertler,Kate Azar,Renée DePencier Piñero,Michael Gingras,Junghyun Kim,Bethany M. Niedzielski,Ilan T. Rosen,Mollie E. Schwartz,Joel Î-j. Wang,Terry P. Orlando,Jeffrey A. Grover,Max Hays,Kyle Serniak,William D. Oliver*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在单个整数磁通量子比特中实现了擦除转换和线路中间擦除检测，通过将|g⟩和|f⟩编码为逻辑态、|e⟩编码为擦除态，并设计消除两个逻辑态之间谐振频率偏移的结构，实现了无需辅助量子比特的线路中间擦除检查。丢弃检测到的擦除事件后，|f⟩态寿命提升8.4倍，Hahn-echo时间提升1.38倍，单量子比特门误差从0.061(2)%降至0.030(5)%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错中擦除错误检测能通过揭示错误事件的时间和位置提高效率，但现有硬件平台在实现高效擦除转换和检测方面存在挑战。本文旨在利用整数磁通量子比特的特殊能级结构，实现硬件高效的擦除错误检测与转换机制。

Method: 利用整数磁通量子比特中|g⟩和|f⟩作为逻辑态、|e⟩作为擦除态的编码方案；通过整数磁通量子比特抑制直接|f⟩→|g⟩跃迁，将主导的|f⟩→|e⟩跃迁转换为可检测擦除；设计消除两个逻辑态间谐振频率偏移的参数空间，利用同一谐振器实现无需辅助量子比特的线路中间擦除检测。

Result: 丢弃检测到的擦除事件后，|f⟩态寿命提升8.4倍，Hahn-echo时间提升1.38倍，单量子比特门误差从0.061(2)%降至0.030(5)%

Conclusion: 整数磁通量子比特是一种硬件高效的擦除错误检测与转换平台，但需进一步改进以实现具有高擦除偏置的有效擦除量子比特。

Abstract: Erasure-error detection can improve the efficiency of quantum error correction by revealing the times and locations of their error events. In this work, we demonstrate erasure conversions and mid-circuit erasure detections in a single integer fluxonium, in which the states $\mathrm{|g\rangle, |f\rangle}$ encode the logical states and $\mathrm{|e\rangle}$ encodes the erasure state. The integer fluxonium suppresses direct $|\mathrm{f} \rangle \rightarrow |\mathrm{g} \rangle$ transitions and allows the dominant $|\mathrm{f} \rangle \rightarrow |\mathrm{e}\rangle$ transitions to be converted into detectable erasures. Furthermore, we identified a design space that nullifies the resonant-frequency shift between the two logical states, enabling ancilla-free mid-circuit erasure checks using the same resonator employed for final readout. By discarding the detected erasure events, we achieved an 8.4-fold increase in the $|\mathrm{f}\rangle$ state lifetime, a 1.38-fold increase in the Hahn-echo time, and a reduction of single-qubit gate error from 0.061(2)% to 0.030(5)%. Our results establish integer fluxonium as a hardware-efficient platform for erasure-error detection and conversion, while identifying the improvements required to realize an effective erasure qubit with high erasure bias.

</details>


### [100] [Parity-Based Time-Bin Encoding Enabling SWAP Between Polarization and Time-Bin Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27144)
*Adam Sultan,Connor Kupchak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于奇偶性的时间仓编码方案，通过偏振控制的延迟线实现偏振与时间仓自由度之间的确定性SWAP操作，并分析了相关误差与分辨率约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统早/晚时间仓编码不支持从晚到早的双向逻辑翻转，限制了某些量子操作的实现。为了在多自由度光子量子处理中实现偏振与时间仓自由度之间的有效路由，需要一种支持双向时间仓翻转的编码方案。

Method: 提出基于奇偶性的时间仓编码，将逻辑|0⟩_T和|1⟩_T对应为时间间隔Δt的偶数倍和奇数倍，使得物理延迟Δt即可实现|0⟩_T ↔ |1⟩_T。利用偏振控制的延迟线实现CNOT_{P→T}，并结合周期性折射率调制实现CNOT_{T→P}，将三个CNOT操作级联实现确定性SWAP。分析了基于场调制的偏振旋转误差概率以及由EOM驱动电子学和光子探测共同决定的时序分辨率约束。

Result: 奇偶性时间仓编码能够支持逻辑时间仓的双向翻转，偏振控制延迟线可自然实现CNOT_{P→T}，周期性折射率调制可实现CNOT_{T→P}，三个CNOT级联构成完整的偏振-时间仓确定性SWAP操作。

Conclusion: 奇偶性时间仓编码是使偏振-时间仓SWAP成为可行的关键要素，该方案为多自由度光子量子处理架构提供了有效的自由度间路由原语。

Abstract: Multi-degree-of-freedom photonic quantum processing requires routing between degree-of-freedom (DOF) qubit encodings on a single photon. A SWAP between polarization and time-bin qubits is Multi-degree-of-freedom photonic quantum processing requires routing between degree-of-freedom (DOF) qubit encodings on a single photon. A SWAP between polarization and time-bin qubits is an advantageous primitive for such architectures, however conventional early/late time-bin encoding does not support bidirectional logical time-bin flips from late to early which limits the ability to implement certain quantum operations. We introduce a parity-based time-bin encoding in which logical $\vert 0 \rangle_T$ and $\vert 1 \rangle_T$ correspond to even and odd multiples of a spacing $Δt$, so that a physical delay of $Δt$ implements $\vert 0 \rangle_T \leftrightarrow \vert 1 \rangle_T$. This encoding is the enabling ingredient that makes a polarization-controlled delay line implement $\mathrm{CNOT}_{P \rightarrow T}$ and aligns naturally with periodic refractive index modulation for $\mathrm{CNOT}_{T \rightarrow P}$. Composing three such CNOT operations sequentially results in a deterministic SWAP between polarization and time-bin degrees of freedom. We analyze field-based modulation polarization-rotation error probability and timing-resolution constraints set by both EOM drive electronics and photon detection.

</details>


### [101] [Improved Methods for Determining Quantum Error Correcting Code Performance and Fault Tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27153)
*Michael Mullan,Matthew Weippert,Winton Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子纠错中逻辑错误率在低错误率区域的性能评估难题，提出两种改进的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法：剪枝算法（去除易纠正错误，聚焦恶性核心）和子区域MCMC（通过参数化每次重采样错误模式的比例，在蒙特卡洛和单步MCMC之间插值），显著加快了收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码在实用化计算所需的低错误率区域的性能无法直接通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估，需要从高错误率外推，但外推依赖未知的最小权重不可纠正错误模式。现有MCMC方法（Bravyi和Vargo）虽比蒙特卡洛高效，但Metropolis算法每次只做微小改变，导致收敛缓慢。

Method: 1. 剪枝算法：识别并移除典型故障模式中大量易纠正的错误，只保留难以纠正的低权重核心。2. 子区域MCMC：一类新的Metropolis-Hastings算法，通过参数控制每次重采样错误模式的比例，在完全蒙特卡洛重采样和单步MCMC之间平滑插值，选择合适的参数可大幅加速收敛。

Result: 子区域MCMC通过合理选择重采样比例参数，收敛速度远优于先前的单步MCMC方法。

Conclusion: 两种新方法（特别是子区域MCMC）能更高效地评估量子纠错码在低错误率区域的性能，为实用化量子计算的纠错方案设计提供了更有效的模拟工具。

Abstract: One of the central challenges in quantum error correction is determining the performance of a code in the low-error regimes needed to implement utility-scale computations. While performance at these error rates is not amenable to direct Monte Carlo simulation, it can be extrapolated from simulations at higher logical error rates, assuming the logical error rate scales predictably with increasing distance or decreasing physical error rate. However, the expected scaling depends sensitively on the minimum weight of uncorrectable error patterns. In many cases, the minimum weight is unknown since it depends not only on the theoretical code distance, but also on details of the implementation. Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods, as adapted to quantum error correction by Bravyi and Vargo, provide a way to estimate logical failure rates in these low-error regimes via simulation. While offering significant gains over Monte Carlo, the described Metropolis algorithm makes small changes to the current logical failure patterns which results in slow convergence. In this paper, we argue that typical failure patterns include a large number of easily correctable errors that coexist alongside a malignant core. This observation motivates two new approaches to better evaluate code performance. First, we describe a pruning algorithm designed to obviate these correctable errors and focus on the problematic low-weight core. Second, we develop a novel family of Metropolis-Hastings algorithms, referred to as subregion MCMC. This technique is parameterized by the fraction of the error pattern that is resampled at each step, effectively interpolating between Monte Carlo and single step MCMC. We show that a judicious choice of this parameter results in far faster convergence than prior work.

</details>


### [102] [Training Quantum Dragons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27168)
*Muhammad Yusf,Bhola Devkota,Mark A. Novotny,Gautam Rupak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次将非平衡格林函数（NEGF）方法量子计算化，通过将NEGF散射问题重构为线性方程组，利用HHL和VQLS算法在量子计算机上计算量子龙纳米器件的透射系数，并在IBM量子处理器上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NEGF是纳米尺度电子输运的标准形式，但经典计算在大规模系统中存在瓶颈。本文旨在利用量子计算的优势，首次实现NEGF的量子计算机实现，以更高效地计算纳米器件的输运性质。

Method: 将NEGF散射问题重构为线性方程组，其解编码透射和反射振幅；应用HHL和VQLS两种量子线性求解算法；采用相似变换对NEGF线性系统进行块对角化，降低块编码矩阵的Pauli分解复杂度；在2位点和6位点量子龙器件上映射到3和4个物理量子比特的紧凑电路。

Result: 在2位点和6位点量子龙器件上成功实现量子计算，进行了理想和含噪声模拟，并在物理IBM量子处理器上完成了实际计算。

Conclusion: 证明了NEGF在量子计算机上实现的可行性，为纳米电子输运的量子模拟开辟了新途径，展示了量子计算在凝聚态物理中的应用潜力。

Abstract: The Non-Equilibrium Green's Function (NEGF) is the standard formalism for nano-scale electron transport. By recasting the NEGF scattering problem as a linear system of equations whose solution encodes the transmission and reflection amplitudes, we present the first quantum computerized implementation of NEGF. We apply both the Harrow--Hassidim--Lloyd and Variational Quantum Linear Solver algorithms to compute the transmission coefficient $T(E)$ of quantum dragon nanodevices within the single-band tight-binding model. Quantum dragon devices exhibit perfect transmission across the full conducting band regardless of internal disorder. The problem maps onto compact circuits of 3 and 4 total physical qubits for the 2-site and 6-site dragon devices, respectively. A similarity transformation block-diagonalizes the NEGF linear system reducing the Pauli decomposition of the block-encoded matrix. We demonstrate the feasibility of quantum computation by performing ideal and noise-aware simulations and computations on physical IBM quantum processor.

</details>


### [103] [OQRAM: Oblivious Quantum Random Access Memory for Securing Delegated Quantum Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27171)
*Shifan Xu,Yizhuo Tan,Yongshan Ding,Jakub Szefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 oblivious QRAM 概念，一种用于隐私保护委托量子查询的密码学抽象，通过加密混洗数据库布局和相干地址掩码技术，在客户端模型中实现安全查询，相比完全盲量子计算大幅降低量子资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准QRAM查询接口在委托设置下会向服务器暴露敏感信息，需要一种保护隐私的委托相干查询访问机制。

Method: 协议包括离线刷新阶段和在线保护查询阶段。数据库以加密混洗布局存储，查询通过基于量子安全伪随机置换(qPRP)或量子一次一密(qOTP)的相干地址掩码保护。为应对恶意服务器，引入诱饵检查实现概率性篡改检测。

Result: 在线保护仅增加适度量子开销，避免指数级量子资源需求；qPRP变体支持多查询场景；与完全盲量子计算相比，实现量子通信的指数级减少。

Conclusion: 该框架为私有委托QRAM访问提供了更轻量的抽象，显著降低客户端和服务端的量子资源需求。

Abstract: Quantum query is a basic subroutine in many quantum algorithms, and Quantum Random Access Memory (QRAM) provides a natural way to realize such coherent query access. In delegated settings, however, a standard QRAM query interface can expose sensitive information to the server. This paper introduces oblivious QRAM, a cryptographic abstraction for privacy-preserving delegated coherent query access. The protocol consists of an offline refresh phase and an online protected query phase. The database is stored in an encrypted and shuffled layout, and each query is protected by coherent address masking using either a quantum-secure pseudorandom permutation (qPRP) based method or a quantum one-time pad (qOTP) based method. In the adopted client model, the online protection adds only modest quantum overhead beyond the query register, avoiding the exponential quantum resources that would otherwise be required by an equivalent local QRAM construction. The qPRP-based variant also supports multi-query use by distributing database refresh across multiple queries to reduce classical communication. To address malicious servers, decoy checks are further incorporated to strengthen privacy protection and enable probabilistic tampering detection. Compared with fully blind quantum computing, this framework provides a lighter abstraction tailored to private delegated QRAM access, significantly reducing quantum resource requirements on both the client and server sides and achieving an exponential reduction in quantum communication.

</details>


### [104] [Classical and Quantum MacWilliams Transforms as Spin Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27173)
*Eric Kubischta,Ian Teixeira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从自旋（spin）的角度统一推导了经典与量子纠错码中MacWilliams变换的Wigner-D旋转形式，揭示了长度变化仅为同一元素在不同自旋表示下的重现，而旋转轴的变化则对应于不同经典/量子理论之间的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示自旋作为MacWilliams变换背后统一的动力学机制，建立经典与量子码字重量枚举理论之间的内在联系。

Method: 从将错误分为平凡和非平凡两类出发，利用运动学方法将MacWilliams变换推导为两个规范基之间的Wigner-D旋转。

Result: 证明长度n变化仅使同一旋转元素出现在自旋n/2表示中；固定n时改变旋转轴即可在不同经典与量子理论之间切换。

Conclusion: 自旋是统一理解经典和量子MacWilliams变换的核心结构，为重量枚举理论提供了简洁的几何框架。

Abstract: Spin is the hidden engine behind the zoo of MacWilliams transforms in weight enumerator theories - not only for qubits and qudits, but even for classical codes. From nothing more than a split into trivial and nontrivial errors, we kinematically derive the MacWilliams transform as a Wigner-$D$ rotation between two canonical bases. Within each classical and quantum theory, changing the length $n$ leaves the rotation untouched: the same element simply reappears at spin $n/2$. And at fixed $n$, changing the rotation axis simply moves between the various classical and quantum theories.

</details>


### [105] [Reconfigurable Optical Platform for One-way Quantum Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27181)
*Francesco Mazzoncini,Hugo Defienne,Romain Alléaume,Sylvain Gigan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于多模光纤和波前整形技术，构建了一种可重构的光学平台，用于实现单向量子通信复杂度协议，并实验验证了指数级的量子-经典通信分离，为实用化量子优势的展示提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算霸权在技术上仍具挑战性，而通信复杂度为利用当前光子平台展示量子优势提供了更有希望的方向，因此需要开发一种实用的可重构硬件平台来实验验证量子通信复杂度优势。

Method: 基于多模光纤和波前整形技术，设计并构建了一个可重构的单向量子通信复杂度光学平台，通过实验实现了一个已知具有指数级量子-经典通信分离的单向量子通信复杂度问题，并结合数值模拟验证了该解码架构对更通用通信任务的适用性。

Result: 成功实验验证了指数级的量子-经典通信分离；数值模拟表明该可重构解码架构能支持更通用的单向通信任务，且可扩展至更高维度而无需增加硬件复杂度。

Conclusion: 多模光纤波前整形是一种通用的单向量子通信复杂度硬件平台，为更强量子-经典分离协议的实用化演示提供了具体路线图。

Abstract: Demonstrating a practical quantum advantage remains a central goal in quantum information science. While quantum computational supremacy is still technologically demanding, communication complexity offers a promising route to showcase quantum advantage with current photonic platforms. Here we introduce a reconfigurable optical platform for one-way quantum communication complexity based on multimode fibers and wavefront shaping. We experimentally validate it by implementing a genuine one-way quantum communication complexity problem for which an exponential quantum--classical communication separation is known. Complementary numerical simulations show that the same reconfigurable decoding architecture can support more general one-way communication tasks with comparable performance, while also offering a route to higher-dimensional implementations without increasing hardware complexity. Together, these results establish multimode-fiber wavefront shaping as a versatile hardware platform for one-way quantum communication complexity and provide a concrete roadmap toward more demanding protocols, where stronger quantum--classical separations could enable practical demonstrations of quantum advantage.

</details>


### [106] [Robust quantum state certification and uncertainty principles for total influence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27184)
*Andrea Coladangelo,Jerry Li,Joseph Slote*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于几乎所有（$1-2^{-Ω(n)}$比例）的$n$量子比特目标态$|ψ\rangle$，仅用非自适应单量子比特Pauli测量即可实现最优的量子态认证，所需样本量$O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ))$在信息论意义上达到最优。其核心技术贡献是布尔函数总影响的加权推广的不确定性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态认证是量子信息处理的核心任务，需要设计高效且实用的协议。现有协议往往需要联合测量或自适应测量，实现难度高。本文试图探索仅用最简单测量（单量子比特Pauli测量）能否达到信息论最优性能，以及这种普适性是否对所有目标态都成立。

Method: 1) 将量子态认证问题转化为布尔函数分析问题；2) 提出加权总影响（weighted total influence）的概念，推广经典布尔函数的Inf算子；3) 证明加权不确定性原理：对于任意布尔函数$f$，其加权总影响与傅里叶变换后的加权总影响之和存在下界；4) 基于该原理设计仅使用单量子比特Pauli测量的非自适应测试协议。

Result: 1) 除指数小比例（$2^{-Ω(n)}$）的目标态外，单量子比特Pauli测量足以实现$\varepsilon$近距与$O(\varepsilon)$远距的区分；2) 测试使用$O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ))$份拷贝即可达到置信度$1-δ$，该样本复杂度在信息论意义下最优（即使允许任意联合测量也无法超越）；3) 证明了未加权版本的不等式：$\mathbf{Inf}[f]+\mathbf{Inf}[\widehat{f}] = Ω(n)$，这是超立方体上的Heisenberg不确定性原理类比。

Conclusion: 单量子比特Pauli测量在几乎所有目标态上都能实现最优量子态认证，极大简化了实验实现要求。加权不确定性原理是一个独立的组合分析工具，有望在量子信息理论和其他领域产生更广泛的应用。

Abstract: We show that nonadaptive single-qubit Pauli measurements suffice to test whether an unknown $n$-qubit state $ρ$ is $\varepsilon$-close to or $O(\varepsilon)$-far from an ideal target state $|ψ\rangle$, for all but a $2^{-Ω(n)}$ fraction of target states. The test uses $O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ))$ copies of $ρ$ to achieve confidence $1-δ$, which is information-theoretically optimal even among protocols with arbitrary joint measurements.
  The main technical innovation is an uncertainty principle for weighted generalizations of the total influence of Boolean functions. As a simple example, the unweighted variant states that $\mathbf{Inf}[f]+\mathbf{Inf}[\widehat{f}] = Ω(n)$, which is a natural hypercube analogue of the Heisenberg uncertainty principle (here $\widehat{\,\cdot\,}$ denotes the $2^{-n/2}$-normalized Fourier transform). The weighted case generalizes $\mathbf{Inf}[\,\cdot\,]$ and $\mathbf{Inf}[\,\widehat{\,\cdot\,}\,]$ to Dirichlet energies associated with Glauber dynamics for certain dual measures on the cube.

</details>


### [107] [Sharp Bounds on Ground State Energy of the SYK Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27185)
*Arpon Basu,Pravesh K. Kothari,Siddhant Midha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK)哈密顿量的算子范数期望，证明了对超常数k ≤ o(√n)有E‖H_SYK‖_op = (1-o(1))·√(2n)/k，并通过引入确定性线性算子x将问题转化为Johnson方案中一个矩阵的谱边问题，给出了严格证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确认Garcia-Garcia、Jia和Verbaarschot在2018年的预测，并回答Feng、Tian和Wei在2019年提出的问题。

Method: 1) 识别一个显式的确定性线性算子x，使得x^(2ℓ)的固定二次型恰好等于SYK哈密顿量的期望迹矩；2) 将x视为超图超边空间上的扭曲玻色子模型；3) 将问题约化为识别x的谱边，该谱边由Johnson方案中的一个自然(n choose k)维矩阵主导，可直接用已知结果计算。

Result: 证明了E‖H_SYK‖_op = (1-o(1))·√(2n)/k对超常数k ≤ o(√n)成立；结果可推广到稀疏SYK哈密顿量；作为推论，Basso-Chen-Dalzell的耗散量子算法对所有k < √n/4可证明地计算SYK基态能量至O(1)乘法因子。

Conclusion: 通过构造具有大二次型的见证态并将之转化为H_SYK最大二次型的下确界证书，证明了所得上界是紧的。

Abstract: We study the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian $H_{\operatorname{SYK}}$ on $n$ Majorana modes with $k$-body interactions, and prove that $\mathbb{E}\|H_{\operatorname{SYK}}\|_{\operatorname{op}} = (1 - o(1))\cdot\sqrt{2n}/k$ for super-constant $k\leq o(\sqrt{n})$, where the expectation is over the disorder variables in the Hamiltonian. This confirms the predictions due to Garcia-Garcia, Jia and Verbaarschot'18 and answers a question posed in Feng, Tian and Wei'19. Our results extend to the sparse SYK Hamiltonian. As a corollary, we obtain that the dissipative quantum algorithm of Basso, Chen and Dalzell'24 provably computes the ground state energy of the SYK Hamiltonian up to an $O(1)$-multiplicative factor for all $k < \sqrt{n}/4$.
  Our key technical idea is identifying an explicit, deterministic linear operator $\mathsf{x}$ such that a fixed quadratic form of $\mathsf{x}^{2\ell}$ exactly equals the expected trace moments of the SYK Hamiltonian for every $n$ and $k$. This linear operator can be naturally viewed as a \emph{twisted} model of bosons on the space of hyperedges of a hypergraph. The problem thus reduces to identifying the spectral edge of $\mathsf{x}$, which we show is dominated by the spectrum of a natural ${n \choose k}$-dimensional matrix from the \emph{Johnson} scheme and is straightforward to compute using known results.
  To show that our bound is sharp, we construct a witness state with a large quadratic form on $\mathsf{x}$ and transform it into a certificate of a lower bound on the largest quadratic form on $H_{\operatorname{SYK}}$.

</details>


### [108] [Very Strong Irreversibility of Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27195)
*Tulja Varun Kondra,Raphael Brinster,Hermann Kampermann,Dagmar Bruß,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子纠缠操控中的不可逆性即使在允许指数级错误的情况下依然存在，解决了Lami和Regula提出的猜想，并进一步在多种操作类别下（非纠缠生成操作、完全PPT保持操作）建立了指数强对偶蒸馏纠缠与纠缠成本之间的严格分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠操控存在根本性的不可逆性：某些混合纠缠态需要纯纠缠来制备，但无法通过局域操作和经典通信（LOCC）恢复出纯纠缠。这种不可逆性即使在最大类别的非纠缠生成操作下依然存在，凸显了纠缠理论与热力学之间的根本区别。作者旨在探究这种不可逆性是否在更宽松的误差条件下仍然成立。

Method: 作者构造了具体的纠缠态族，证明任何试图恢复可逆性的尝试必然产生随副本数指数增长的错误。技术上，他们展示了指数强对偶蒸馏纠缠与指数强对偶纠缠成本之间的严格分离。对于非纠缠操作，推导了指数强对偶成本的半定规划下界；对于完全PPT保持操作，构造了可解析求解的反对称态族。

Result: 解决了Lami和Regula的猜想并加强：纠缠不可逆性即使在多项式增长误差（随副本数）下依然存在。建立了指数强对偶蒸馏纠缠与成本之间的严格分离。在完全PPT保持操作下，构造了反对称态族展示指数强对偶不可逆性。值得注意的是，即使在更受限的LOCC操作下，目前也尚未发现类似的分离结果。

Conclusion: 量子纠缠的不可逆性是一种极其坚固的现象，即使在允许指数级或多项式级误差的条件下也无法被克服。这一结果深化了我们对纠缠资源理论本质的理解，表明它与热力学之间存在根本性的差异。

Abstract: The manipulation of quantum entanglement is fundamentally irreversible: some mixed entangled states require pure entanglement for their preparation, although no pure entanglement can be recovered from them by local operations and classical communication. This irreversibility is known to persist even under the maximal class of operations that do not generate entanglement, revealing a fundamental distinction between entanglement theory and thermodynamics. We construct cases for which any attempt to restore reversibility necessarily incurs an error that increases exponentially with the number of copies. Technically, we demonstrate a strict separation between the exponential strong-converse distillable entanglement and the exponential strong-converse entanglement cost. Our result resolves a conjecture posed by Lami and Regula (Nat. Phys. 19, 184-189 (2023)) and strengthens it by showing that the irreversibility of entanglement persists even at the level of polynomially (in the number of copies) growing error. We further derive a semidefinite-programming lower bound on the exponential strong-converse cost under non-entangling operations. Finally, for the class of completely PPT-preserving operations, we construct analytically solvable families of antisymmetric states exhibiting the exponential strong-converse irreversibility. Remarkably, to our knowledge, no analogous separation between exponential strong converse cost and the analogous distillable entanglement is currently known even under the more restrictive class of LOCC operations.

</details>


### [109] [Fault-Tolerant Logical Operations and Efficient State Preparation in Modular Quantum Architectures with Noisy Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27204)
*Siddardha Chelluri,Riccardo Mengoni,Tom Darras,Julien Laurat,Eleni Diamanti,Ioannis Lavdas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过电路级仿真研究了基于旋转表面码的模块化量子计算架构中，模块接口噪声与内部噪声对容错逻辑门的影响，发现接口可容忍比内部高一个数量级的噪声；同时提出了一种高效制备分布式容错逻辑GHZ态的协议，将辅助量子比特最小化问题转化为图上的顶点覆盖问题，并给出多项式时间启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化量子计算是突破单片量子设备资源限制的重要范式，但模块间接口的噪声特性及其对容错计算的影响尚不明确，需要系统研究接口噪声容忍度及高效的分布式纠缠态制备方案。

Method: 采用旋转表面码进行量子比特编码，通过电路级仿真模拟基于晶格手术的容错非局域CNOT门操作，分析在不同噪声水平下的逻辑错误率；将分布式容错逻辑GHZ态制备中的辅助量子比特最小化问题建模为图上顶点覆盖问题，并提出多项式时间启发式算法求解。

Result: 接口噪声可容忍比内部噪声高一个数量级，且对容错阈值的影响较小；提出的协议能有效降低辅助量子比特开销、时间和非局域Bell对的消耗。

Conclusion: 分布式量子纠错能够在模块化架构中实现可扩展的容错量子计算，为模块化量子计算的实际部署提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Modular quantum computing is a leading paradigm for scaling quantum computation beyond the resource limitations of monolithic devices. In this architecture, multiple quantum processing units (QPUs), employing identical or distinct qubit modalities, are interconnected via shared entanglement. Here, we investigate how errors at module interfaces and within individual QPUs affect fault-tolerant computation when qubits are encoded using the rotated surface code. Going beyond the logical-memory benchmark, we perform circuit-level simulations of fault-tolerant nonlocal CNOT gates implemented via lattice surgery between QPUs connected by noisy Bell pairs, and analyze the resulting logical error rates. Our results show that interfaces can tolerate noise up to an order of magnitude higher than intra-QPU noise, with only a minor reduction in the fault-tolerance threshold. We further develop an efficient protocol for preparing distributed fault-tolerant logical GHZ states, reducing ancilla overhead, time, and nonlocal Bell-pair consumption. We show that ancilla minimization in this setting is equivalent to a vertex-cover problem on an associated graph, and introduce a polynomial-time heuristic algorithm for finding low-overhead solutions. Our results provide quantitative evidence that distributed quantum error correction can enable scalable, fault-tolerant quantum computation in modular architectures.

</details>


### [110] [Practical Quantum Topological Data Analysis with Applications to High-Dimensional Feature Extraction and Time Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27206)
*Jason Iaconis,Sayonee Ray,Samwel Sekwao,Claudio Girotto,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将量子拓扑数据分析（TDA）作为一种特征提取方法，通过提取组合拉普拉斯算子的低阶谱信息作为高维拓扑的代理，在两个时间序列应用中展示了高阶TDA特征提升预测性能，并开发了基于矩的量子算法，在离子阱量子系统上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典TDA计算高维拓扑特征的计算成本高昂，而现有量子TDA算法通常专注于精确或高精度的Betti数估计，导致实用的量子优势区间看似狭窄。作者旨在将量子TDA重新定位为下游数据分析的特征提取方法，以拓宽其实用性。

Method: 1) 在应用层面，将高阶TDA特征用于功能MRI分析和金融时间序列分析两项任务；2) 在算法层面，开发基于矩的量子算法，提取低阶矩（包括相对迹）作为高维Betti信息的代理；3) 在实验层面，在类似于IonQ Tempo的钡离子阱系统上提取拉普拉斯可观测量，并与精确Betti信息进行定量比较。

Result: 高阶TDA特征在神经退行性疾病分类和金融市场不稳定性识别中提升了预测性能；低阶矩与高维Betti信息强相关，即使相对Betti数较小时也是如此；在离子阱量子系统上成功提取了拉普拉斯可观测量并与精确Betti信息一致。

Conclusion: 量子TDA可以作为从经典难处理数据中提取拓扑特征的实用方法，从精确Betti数估计转向基于矩的特征提取策略，拓展了量子优势的实用场景。

Abstract: Topological data analysis (TDA) provides a powerful framework for extracting information about the shape of complex, unstructured data, but the classical cost of computing high dimensional topological features limits its application. Quantum algorithms for TDA offer a route around this bottleneck, yet existing approaches typically focus on exact or high precision Betti number estimation, making the regime for practical quantum advantage appear narrow. Here, we instead frame quantum TDA as a feature-extraction method for downstream data analysis by extracting low-order spectral information from the combinatorial Laplacian as a proxy for high-dimensional topology.
  We support this perspective from both the application and algorithmic sides. First, we show that higher-order TDA features improve predictive performance in two time-series applications: functional MRI analysis for neurodegenerative disease classification and financial time-series analysis for identifying market instability. Second, we develop a moment-based quantum algorithm and show that low-order moments, including the relative trace, are strongly correlated with high-dimensional Betti information, even when the relative Betti number is small. Finally, we present circuit constructions, resource estimates, quantum-classical crossover projections, and experimental results from a Barium development system similar to the forthcoming IonQ Tempo line, extracting Laplacian-derived observables from graph instances and quantitatively comparing them with exact Betti information.
  Together, these results establish quantum TDA as a practical approach for extracting topological features from classically challenging data

</details>

### [111] [Gravitational Waves from Multiple Cosmic Superstrings and the Overshoot Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26124)
*Luca Brunelli,Michele Cicoli,Muhammad Hassan,Seyed Ehsan Qoreishi,Francisco Gil Pedro*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了后暴胀弦宇宙学中多个宇宙超弦种群的演化动力学，发现由NS5-和D3-膜缠绕内循环产生的有效弦早期衰变产生的引力波辐射背景阻止了模场越过最小值，而基本弦存活更久并在模场振荡期间衰变，贡献约1/3的能量密度。这些多宇宙超弦衰变产生的引力波谱即使在模场主导后期被稀释，仍可产生高频多峰信号，为弦理论提供可观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究后暴胀弦宇宙学中多个宇宙超弦种群的动力学行为，探索其引力波信号能否作为弦理论的可观测特征。

Method: 对模场在陡峭势能上滚动至晚期最小值的动力学系统进行完整分析，考虑NS5-和D3-膜缠绕内循环产生的有效弦及基本弦的衰变过程，以及引力波辐射背景的摩擦效应。

Result: NS5-和D3-膜产生的有效弦早期衰变产生的引力波辐射背景阻止了模场越过最小值；基本弦存活更久，在模场振荡期间衰变并贡献约1/3的总能量密度；多超弦衰变产生的引力波谱呈高频多峰结构。

Conclusion: 即使经模场主导后期稀释，多超弦衰变的高频多峰引力波信号仍可作为弦理论一般特征的可观测标志。

Abstract: Post inflationary string cosmology can feature an initial population of multiple species of cosmic superstrings whose tension is controlled by a modulus rolling over a steep potential toward a late-time minimum. We perform a full analysis of the associated dynamical system, finding that overshooting the minimum is prevented by the friction of a radiation background of gravitational waves produced from the early decay of effective strings arising from NS5- and D3-branes wrapped around internal cycles. On the other hand, fundamental strings survive longer and decay when the modulus is oscillating around the minimum and they have about $1/3$ of the total energy density. The spectrum of gravitational waves generated by the decays of these multiple cosmic superstrings, even if diluted by a late epoch of modulus domination, can still result in a high-frequency, multi-peaked signal, offering an observational signature of generic features of string theory.

</details>


### [112] [Iterative Gauging is Deconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26126)
*Sebastian Franco,Diego Rodriguez-Gomez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现，将量子场论的对称性进行迭代规范可以生成一个额外的空间维度，其根本机制是维度解构（dimensional deconstruction）——即通过箭图规范理论编码额外维度的过程。通过对偶化希格斯支上的戈德斯通场，标准解构作用与迭代规范作用完全等价；箭图节点对应规范场，箭图链接对应耦合磁对称性的规范场。从规范电对称性出发则得到对偶变量下的解构理论，所有耦合常数被反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示迭代规范对称性产生额外空间维度的精确机制，并将其与已知的维度解构过程建立严格对应关系。

Method: 通过分析标准解构作用与迭代规范作用的数学等价性，建立箭图节点/链接与规范场之间的对应关系，并分别考察规范电对称性和磁对称性两种情况的对偶变换。

Result: 标准解构作用与迭代规范作用完全等价；箭图节点对应规范场，链接对应耦合磁对称性的规范场；规范电对称性得到对偶变量下的解构理论且耦合常数反转。

Conclusion: 迭代规范对称性生成额外维度的机制就是维度解构，两者通过明确的字典完全对应。

Abstract: A recent construction showed that iteratively gauging symmetries of a quantum field theory can generate an emergent extra spatial dimension. Here we identify the precise mechanism underlying this phenomenon: it is dimensional deconstruction, the procedure by which an extra dimension is encoded in the structure of a quiver gauge theory. We demonstrate this by showing that the standard deconstruction action, upon dualizing the Goldstone fields on the Higgs branch, is exactly equivalent to the action produced by iterative gauging. The dictionary is transparent: quiver nodes correspond to gauge fields, and quiver links correspond to the gauge fields introduced to couple to magnetic symmetries. We further show that starting by gauging the electric rather than the magnetic symmetry produces the deconstructed theory written in the dual variables, with all coupling constants inverted.

</details>


### [113] [CasimirRFM: A Mathematica package for Riemann-flat compactifications with Casimir energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26140)
*Bruno Valeixo Bento*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: CasimirRFM是一个Mathematica软件包，用于研究黎曼平坦流形上的紧致化，并计算高维场论和超引力中的单圈Casimir能量。它利用Ewald求和对格点求和进行高效数值评估，计算低维Casimir势和局域高维Casimir能量密度，支持一般无质量谱和扭曲边界条件。该软件包提供了构建和分析定义这些流形的有限群、确定不变度量和上同调基、识别相容的旋量结构以及计算模空间度量的工具。此外，它还评估引力子、p-形式、旋量和Rarita-Schwinger表示中完整群元素的迹。我们描述了该软件包的数学表述和数值实现，并通过Type IIB超引力在T^6/Z_8上的紧致化示例说明其应用，包括Casimir势的计算和局域化Casimir-膜贡献的可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维场论和超引力在黎曼平坦流形上的紧致化是弦理论和额外维模型的重要研究方向，但Casimir能量的精确数值计算面临格点求和高计算成本、复杂边界条件处理等挑战，现有工具缺乏系统化支持。

Method: 开发CasimirRFM Mathematica软件包，采用Ewald求和对格点求和进行高效数值评估；提供有限群理论的构造与分析、不变度量和上同调基的计算、相容旋量结构的识别、模空间度量的计算等功能；实现引力子、p-形式、旋量和Rarita-Schwinger表示中完整群元素迹的评估。

Result: 成功实现了一个支持黎曼平坦流形紧致化单圈Casimir能量计算的数值软件包，并通过Type IIB超引力在T^6/Z_8上的紧致化案例展示了Casimir势的计算和局域化Casimir-膜贡献的可视化。

Conclusion: CasimirRFM为高维场论和超引力的紧致化研究提供了一个系统化的数值计算工具，能够高效处理复杂流形上的Casimir能量计算，有助于深入理解额外维物理和超引力理论。

Abstract: CasimirRFM is a Mathematica package for the study of compactifications on Riemann-flat manifolds and the computation of one-loop Casimir energies in higher-dimensional field theories and supergravity. It implements an efficient numerical evaluation of lattice sums using Ewald summation, and computes both lower-dimensional Casimir potentials and local higher-dimensional Casimir energy densities, allowing for general massless spectra and twisted boundary conditions. The package provides tools for constructing and analysing the finite groups defining these manifolds, determining invariant metrics and cohomology bases, identifying compatible spin structures, and computing moduli-space metrics. Moreover, it evaluates the traces of holonomy elements in the graviton, $p$-form, spinor, and Rarita-Schwinger representations. We describe the mathematical formulation and numerical implementation of the package, and illustrate its use through a compactification of Type IIB supergravity on $T^6/\mathbb{Z}_8$, including the computation of the Casimir potential and the visualization of localised Casimir-brane contributions.

</details>


### [114] [Toward an Observable Algebra for JT-de Sitter Space: Gap Protection and Modular Dressings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26194)
*Hassan ElSayed*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全局德西特时空中基本补集与区域算子代数之间的关系。在dS₂中，基本补集由最大互补间隙决定：仅当间隙角度≥π时起作用，且至多一个间隙有效。高维情形对应包含开半球。在Möbius共形手征网中，区域代数通过共享辅助时钟与真空模流交叉，推导了连续核包含的精确判据。引入表示依赖的交换子模型，发现保护间隙重叠参考弧时指派代数为Type-II₁真子代数，而极短反足分量会消除基本补集并导致不连续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全局德西特空间中基本补集与区域算子代数的联系，为全息原理和德西特量子引力中的代数量子场论提供严格基础。

Method: 在dS₂上分析开弧并集的几何条件（最大间隙角度≥π）；推广到高维（包含开半球）；在抽象Möbius共形手征网中引入共享辅助时钟交叉区域代数与真空模流；构造表示依赖的交换子模型。

Result: dS₂中基本补集由唯一≥π的互补间隙决定；连续核包含的精确判据因真空关联受阻；保护间隙重叠参考弧时得到Type-II₁真子代数；短反足分量消除基本补集导致代数不连续变化。

Conclusion: 建立了德西特时空几何与区域算子代数结构之间的清晰对应关系，揭示了全息学中区域代数的非平凡依赖性和不连续性。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between fundamental complements and regional operator algebras in global de Sitter space. For a finite union of open arcs on the time-symmetric circle of global dS$_2$, we show that the fundamental complement is determined by the largest complementary gap: a gap contributes precisely when its angular length is at least π, and at most one gap can do so. In higher-dimensional global de Sitter space, the corresponding criterion is containment of an open hemisphere.
  We then study the operator-algebraic consequences in an abstract Möbius-covariant chiral conformal net. Each regional algebra is crossed with its vacuum modular flow using a shared auxiliary clock. We derive an exact criterion for inclusions between the resulting continuous cores and show that vacuum correlations obstruct this criterion when the larger region contains an additional spacelike arc. A compatible family can nevertheless be obtained on the finite Boolean algebra generated by a fixed collection of separated arcs by using a split product state.
  Motivated by the holograms prescription of Bousso and Penington, we introduce a representation-dependent commutant model for regional algebras. When the protected gap overlaps a fixed reference arc, the assigned algebra is a proper von Neumann subalgebra of the reference Type-II$_1$ factor. For an explicit two-arc family, adding an arbitrarily short antipodal component removes the fundamental complement and changes the assigned algebra discontinuously to the full reference algebra.

</details>


### [115] [Intersection Bounds for BPS Strings in Six-Dimensional Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26197)
*Hee-Cheol Kim,Kai Xu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从有效场论角度出发，利用Zariski分解（视为电荷格点上的吸引子机制）与E-弦世界sheet理论中的当前代数嵌入，推导了六维𝒩=(1,0)超引力中张量模空间内原始BPS弦电荷生成元及其交配数的普适上界，补全了此前关于自交数-2和-3生成元的缺失边界，并证明了张量电荷交配数在对偶意义下的有限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六维𝒩=(1,0)超引力中，张量模空间的结构由原始BPS弦电荷（BPS生成元）及其交配决定。虽然规范反常消除约束了支撑规范代数的生成元之间的交点，但对于与自交数为-2和-3的E-弦相交的生成元，其交点数的边界此前尚不完整。本文旨在从纯有效场论视角补全这些缺失的边界条件。

Method: 将Zariski分解重新解释为电荷格点版本的吸引子机制，结合E-弦世界sheet理论中的当前代数嵌入，从有效场论出发推导出交点数的普适上界。该结构最初通过AI引导发现，后经EFT论证得到严格分析证明。

Result: 得到了关于E-弦与自交数-2、-3生成元之间交点数的强普适边界，从而建立了张量电荷交配数直至对偶等价的有限性结果。

Conclusion: Zariski分解作为吸引子机制在电荷格点上的对应，结合世界sheet理论约束，可完全从EFT角度确定六维超引力中张量模空间的交配数边界，证明了交配数的有限性。

Abstract: In six-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=(1,0)$ supergravity, the structure of tensor moduli space is governed by primitive BPS string charges known as BPS generators and their intersection pairing. We derive bounds on the intersection numbers of these generators from a purely effective field theory (EFT) perspective. Although gauge anomaly cancellation constrains intersections between generators supporting gauge algebras, bounds for E-strings intersecting generators with self-intersection numbers $-2$ and $-3$ have previously remained incomplete. We show that the Zariski decomposition, interpreted as the charge lattice counterpart of the attractor mechanism, together with current algebra embeddings on the E-string worldsheet theory, yields strong universal bounds on these intersection numbers. These results establish the finiteness of tensor charge intersection numbers up to duality. The underlying structure was identified through AI-guided investigation and is proven here analytically using EFT arguments.

</details>


### [116] [Nucleon spectra and wave functions from holographic models with dual Einstein-dilaton and Starobinsky-dilaton gravities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26319)
*Adão S. da Silva Junior,Juan M. Z. Pretel,Henrique Boschi-Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-狄拉克引力和斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉克引力两种全息框架下研究核子谱，通过引入新参数改进与实验数据的对比，并提出了基于斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉克引力的新全息模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进爱因斯坦-狄拉克全息QCD模型对核子谱的描述，并探索斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉克引力在全息QCD中的应用，以更好地拟合实验数据。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-狄拉克全息模型中引入新参数以改进核子谱与实验数据的对比；然后将该思路扩展到斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉克引力，构建新的改进全息模型，并重新分析核子谱。

Result: 通过引入新参数，改进的爱因斯坦-狄拉克模型能更好地拟合实验核子谱数据；新提出的斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉克全息模型也可用于核子谱分析。

Conclusion: 两种全息引力模型均可用于描述核子谱，斯塔罗宾斯基-狄拉克模型可作为软-wall模型和爱因斯坦-狄拉克模型之外的新选择。

Abstract: We study the nucleon spectra in two holographic set-ups: Einstein-dilaton and Starobinsky-dilaton gravity models. The Einstein-dilaton holographic model, also known as improved holographic QCD, have been proposed some time ago to describe confinement and glueball spectra. Recently, it has been applied to the case of mesons and nucleons. In this work, we reconsider the Einstein-dilaton holographic model to discuss the nucleon spectra introducing new parameters that allow us to improve the comparison with experimental data. Then, we extend this idea to the context of Starobinsky-dilaton gravity defining another improved holographic model. We use this new holographic model to reanalyse the nucleon spectra and also compare them with soft-wall model and experimental data.

</details>


### [117] [D-branes and nonlinear interactions in the $AdS$ pure spinor string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26343)
*Brenno Carlini Vallilo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了一种坐标无关的代数框架，用于描述闭弦通道中纯旋量弦在AdS₅×S⁵上的零场边界条件，通过psu(2,2|4)的对合自同构构造了七种Lorentzian零通量D膜几何，并导出了类似Dirac-Born-Infeld的边界相互作用一致性方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为纯旋量弦形式下AdS₅×S⁵背景的闭弦通道提供一种坐标无关、代数化的边界条件描述方法，从而系统地构造候选的half-BPS、Lorentzian D膜几何。

Method: 利用psu(2,2|4)的对合自同构（交换Z₄分次的奇部）组织构造；通过固定子代数得到候选D膜几何；构建物质与鬼边界相互作用的局域ansatz，推导切向超联络、横向嵌入及四个鬼胶合耦合的一致性方程。

Result: 得到七种代表元，实现Lorentzian零通量D1、D3、D5及D7探针扇区；导出的方程组构成膜世界体上的Dirac-Born-Infeld型运动方程。

Conclusion: 该代数框架为AdS₅×S⁵上纯旋量弦的边界条件提供了系统化的构造方法，所得方程与DBI理论一致，为研究AdS/CFT中的D膜探针提供了严格的形式化基础。

Abstract: We develop a coordinate-independent algebraic description of zero-field boundary conditions for the pure spinor string on $AdS_5\times S^5$ in the closed-string channel. The construction is organized by involutive automorphisms of $\mathfrak{psu}(2,2|4)$ that exchange the odd sectors of the $\mathbb Z_4$ grading. Their fixed subalgebras provide candidate half-BPS, Lorentzian D-brane geometries with vanishing background world-volume flux, consistent with the standard $AdS_2$, $AdS_3\times S^1$, $AdS_4\times S^2$, and related embeddings. Together, the seven representatives realize the Lorentzian zero-flux D1, D3, D5, and D7 probe sectors considered here. We then construct a local ansatz for the matter and ghost boundary interactions and derive a system of consistency equations for the tangential superconnection, transverse embedding, and four ghost-gluing couplings. The resulting system is a set of Dirac-Born-Infeld-like equations of motion on the brane world-volume.

</details>


### [118] [Generalized Bergshoeff-de Roo identification in the Supergravity frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26415)
*Walter H. Baron,Fabian A. Portilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文回顾并改进了广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别方法，利用微分同胚、洛伦兹、规范不变性和T对偶性，在超引力框架下直接推导弦理论有效作用量中的高导数耦合项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦理论有效作用量具有高度约束的结构，需要利用底层理论的众多对称性来推导高导数耦合项。广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别方法最初在双场论框架下发展，本文旨在将该方法回溯并改进，使其能直接在标准超引力框架下生成耦合项。

Method: 利用微分同胚不变性、洛伦兹不变性、规范不变性以及T对偶性等对称性，通过广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别方法，在双场论框架的基础上进一步改进，直接在标准超引力框架中推导高导数耦合项。

Result: 成功将广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别方法从双场论框架回溯到标准超引力框架，实现了直接在超引力中生成高导数耦合项。

Conclusion: 对广义Bergshoeff-de Roo识别方法的改进使其能够直接在标准超引力框架下推导高导数耦合项，为弦理论有效作用量的研究提供了更直接的途径。

Abstract: The effective action of string theory has a highly constrained structure due to the numerous symmetries of the underlying theory. Recently, a method known as the Generalized Bergshoeff-de Roo identification was developed, which exploits diffeomorphism, Lorentz and gauge invariance, along with T-duality, to derive higher-derivative couplings in the effective action within the framework of Double Field Theory. This method has been revisited and further refined to generate couplings directly within the standard Supergravity framework.

</details>


### [119] [Massive and massless particles in Mielke--Baekler geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26532)
*Carles Batlle,Roberto Casalbuoni,Daniele Dominici,José Figueroa-O'Farrill,Joaquim Gomis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维Mielke-Baekler几何（具有挠率的洛伦兹齐性时空）中的有质量与无质量自旋粒子世界线作用量，特别关注曲率消失的遥平行分支（反德西特时空），通过Wess-Zumino项引入自旋后发现了具有不同自由度数的动力学扇区（正则扇区和临界扇区），并分析了无质量粒子的诺特对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将闵可夫斯基和（反）德西特时空推广到允许挠率存在的三维洛伦兹齐性时空（Mielke-Baekler几何），并构造在该类时空中运动的带自旋粒子的世界线作用量，以理解挠率对粒子动力学的影响。

Method: 利用余伴随轨道和非线性实现技术构造世界线作用量；通过引入Wess-Zumino项描述自旋；区分正则扇区和临界扇区分析动力学自由度；利用诺特定理分析对称性。

Result: 1) 反德西特时空是除闵可夫斯基时空外唯一允许不变Weitzenböck连接（平坦有挠）的洛伦兹流形；2) 自旋项的引入导致出现正则扇区和临界扇区，两者物理自由度数目不同；3) 有质量粒子情形下，临界扇区出现Papapetrou型强迫项；4) 无质量自旋粒子在正则扇区中只有Killing子集是诺特变换，临界扇区中共形Killing贡献可通过规范变换消去。

Conclusion: 成功在具有挠率的三维Mielke-Baekler时空中构建了带自旋粒子的世界线作用量，揭示了不同动力学扇区的存在及其物理含义，阐明了挠率与对称性破缺之间的关系。

Abstract: The Mielke--Baekler geometries are three-dimensional
  reductive homogeneous spacetimes together with a choice of invariant
  connection which is compatible with a lorentzian metric. The
  spacetimes generalise Minkowski and (anti)de~Sitter spacetimes in
  that the invariant metric connection can have torsion, a peculiarity
  of three dimensions. Using coadjoint orbits and the techniques of
  nonlinear realisations, we construct worldline actions for massive
  and massless spinning particles moving in these spacetimes. We pay
  particular attention to the so-called teleparallel branch, in which
  the curvature of the invariant connection vanishes. Apart from the trivial Minkowski case, this singles
  out anti-de~Sitter spacetime, as the only of these lorentzian
  manifolds admitting an invariant Weitzenböck connection; that is, a
  flat connection with torsion. The introduction of a Wess--Zumino
  term describing spin has, as a main consequence, the appearance of
  dynamical sectors (denoted ``regular'' and ``critical''), with a
  different number of physical degrees of freedom. In particular, in
  the massive case, we discuss the formulation of the dynamics in
  terms of either the Weitzenböck or the Levi-Civita connection, and
  the emergence of a Papapetrou-type forcing term in the critical sector.
  For the massless particle we study the Noether
  symmetries of the action, which, for the spinless case, include the
  conformal transformations. For nonzero spin, only the Killing subset
  survives as genuine Noether transformations in the regular sector,
  while in the critical sector any conformal Killing contribution can
  be set to zero by a gauge transformation.

</details>


### [120] [Static topological dilatonic black hole with multiple horizons and its thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26535)
*M. M. Stetsko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在爱因斯坦-标量-非线性电磁理论中获得了静态拓扑带电黑洞解，该解具有任意数量视界、丰富的热力学行为（包括三重点和稳定相变），并通过扩展相空间方法得到了Smarr关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论中黑洞最多只有两个视界，作者希望引入非线性电磁场来获得具有更丰富视界结构和热力学行为的黑洞解。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-标量理论中引入由麦克斯韦场不变量的无穷级数表示的非线性电磁场，推导静态拓扑带电黑洞解；利用欧几里得方法研究正则系综和巨正则系综的热力学性质；采用扩展相空间方法（将宇宙常数视为热力学变量）分析近临界行为并推导Smarr关系。

Result: 得到了具有任意数量视界的黑洞解；非线性电磁场本身满足所有能量条件，而标量场仅满足零能量条件；热力学行为比标准EMD解更丰富，包括可能存在的额外稳定相和三重点；不同方法得到的热力学结果一致。

Conclusion: 非线性电磁场的引入使得黑洞解具有更丰富的视界结构和热力学相变行为，包括三重点和多稳定相；全局稳定性与标准EMD解或Reissner-Nordström黑洞有共同特征，但也因非线性场而出现新的特性。

Abstract: We obtain a static topological charged black hole solution within Einstein-dilaton theory with nonlinear electromagnetic field represented by an infinite series over Maxwell field invariant. In contrast with standard Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton (EMD) theory where the black hole might have no more than two horizons, the nonlinear generalization might give rise to a solution with arbitrary number of horizons. We examine energy conditions and show that for the nonlinear electromagnetic field itself all the energy conditions are fulfilled outside of the black hole, whereas for the dilaton field the only condition which is not violated outside is the Null Energy Condition (NEC). We also study thermodynamics of the black hole and show that the thermal behaviour of the solution is considerably richer than for its standard EMD cousin. To have complete description of the thermodynamics we also use the Euclidean method, which allows us to consider the so-called Grand Canonical and Canonical Ensembles. The results obtained by different approaches show their consistency. The Euclidean description naturally allowed us to consider global stability, which is shown to have some common features with standard EMD solution, or even Reissner-Nordström black hole. But there some new peculiarities caused by the nonlinear field, namely we might have additional stable phases and a triple point. To examine near critical behaviour we consider the extended phase space approach, where the cosmological constant is supposed to be a thermodynamic variable. Making use of the extended formalism we also obtain the Smarr relation.

</details>


### [121] [World-Line Actions in Weyl Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26868)
*Cezar Condeescu,Andrei Micu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文从规范场论角度构建了Weyl几何中粒子在类时曲线上运动的world-line作用量。该作用量无量纲、Weyl不变、可加但通常非局域，需要通过开放Wilson线来处理一般Weyl场。研究表明在对称相中，由于无法同时满足仿射参数化、时间维度、可加性和Weyl不变性，不存在令人满意的固有时概念；只有在自发对称破缺后粒子获得质量，作用量变为Riemann几何形式，固有时才能重新定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在Weyl几何中是否存在满足Weyl不变性的固有时定义，以及如何从规范场论角度构建粒子的world-line作用量。

Method: 从规范场论视角构建Weyl几何中粒子的world-line作用量，引入开放Wilson线处理非局域性，并通过einbein构造经典等价的二次作用量，展示非局域性源于约束场的积分。

Result: 构建了Weyl几何中粒子的world-line作用量，证明对称相中无法同时满足固有时定义的四个条件（仿射参数化、时间维度、可加性、Weyl不变性），因而没有满意的固有时概念；只有在自发对称破缺后才能在Riemann几何中重新定义固有时。

Conclusion: Weyl几何的对称相中不存在合理的固有时定义，这需要自发对称破缺和粒子获得质量后才能实现。

Abstract: In this note we construct, from a gauge theory perspective, the world-line action for a particle moving on a time-like curve in Weyl geometry. The action we find is dimensionless, Weyl invariant, additive and, in general, non-local due to an open Wilson line which we have to add in order to account for a general Weyl field. In special cases, this Wilson line can be local, but the geometry becomes integrable. The action can not be used to measure the proper time as it is dimensionless and no mass parameter is allowed in the symmetric phase of the theory. We show that the usual conditions for defining proper time: affine parametrization, dimension of time and additivity supplemented by the requirement of Weyl invariance can not be fulfilled simultaneously and therefore no satisfactory notion of proper time exists in the symmetric phase. Under spontaneous symmetry breaking the particle acquires a mass, the action becomes Riemannian and the proper time can be again defined. We also construct a classically equivalent quadratic action by using an einbein on the world-line and show that the non-locality can be seen to arise from integrating out a constrained field.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum Fisher information of the Klein--Gordon, $φ^4$, and Dirac vacua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26919)
*Preslav Asenov,Alessio Serafini*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文计算了三种量子场论（Klein-Gordon场、φ⁴相互作用场论、自由Dirac场）的真空态关于质量参数的量子Fisher信息，分析了不同时空维度下的标度行为与发散特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子Fisher信息作为量子场论中参数估计精度的度量，揭示真空态中质量参数信息含量的维度依赖性与普适标度行为。

Method: 在(d+1)维欧氏时空下，对Klein-Gordon场解析计算QFI；对φ⁴理论在相互作用常数一阶微扰下计算QFI；对自由Dirac场计算QFI。通过维度分析考察各理论的标度律与发散结构。

Result: Klein-Gordon场QFI呈m^{d-2}依赖，d=2时为零（与全息对偶一致）；φ⁴相互作用在d=3导致QFI发散，在d=0减少可用信息；自由Dirac场QFI在d=2,3时紫外发散，d=1时质量依赖，d=0时为零。

Conclusion: 量子Fisher信息揭示了不同场论真空态中质量信息的维度依赖特性，微扰相互作用会改变信息结构，某些维度下出现发散或零信息行为，与全息对偶等理论预言一致。

Abstract: The quantum Fisher information (QFI) of the vacuum of three quantum field theories is evaluated with respect to the mass parameter of each theory. All field theories are considered on a $(d+1)$-dimensional Euclidean spacetime. We consider the Klein-Gordon field, a quartic interaction ($φ^4$) field theory, and the free Dirac field. In the case of the quartic interaction theory, the QFI is given perturbatively to first order in the interaction constant. For the free KG case, we find a $m^{d-2}$ dependence of the QFI, and thus no dependence for $d=2$, in agreement with the holographic duality characterizing the theory. The introduction of a quartic interaction is shown to lead to a QFI divergence in $d=3$ and to reduce the available information in the $d=0$ system. The vacuum QFI with respect to the free Dirac field mass is shown to be UV-divergent for $d=2$ and $d=3$, mass-dependent for $d=1$, and zero for $d=0$.

</details>


### [123] [Extended Phase Space Thermodynamics and Joule-Thomson Expansion of Regular AdS Black Holes in a String Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26931)
*Y. Elaima,A. Daassou,H. Lekbich,F. Oubbad*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究浸入弦云背景中的静态球对称规则黑洞的扩展相空间热力学，发现量子启发的规则化核心和弦云密度显著影响黑洞热稳定性，并揭示了与平均场理论一致的临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索规则黑洞在弦云背景下的热力学行为，特别是负宇宙常数作为热力学压力时，规则化核心和弦云参数对热稳定性、临界现象的影响。

Method: 采用扩展相空间热力学方法，将负宇宙常数视为热力学压力；分析临界压缩比、标度不变性、临界指数；利用焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀定义等焓加热和冷却区域。

Result: 规则化核心具有严格的标度不变性；弦云参数显著改变范德瓦耳斯临界压缩比；临界指数与平均场理论一致；明确了等焓加热/冷却区域。

Conclusion: 该非奇异时空下，规则化核心和弦云背景共同调控黑洞热力学行为，焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀为热力学表征提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We investigate the extended phase space thermodynamics of a static, spherically symmetric regular black hole immersed in a string cloud background. By identifying the negative cosmological constant as a thermodynamic pressure, our analysis indicates that the quantum-inspired regularizing core and the string cloud density significantly affect the thermal stability of the black hole. We specifically illustrate a stringent scale invariance linked to the regularizing core, while the dimensionless string cloud parameter substantially alters the universal Van der Waals critical compressibility ratio. The significant geometric inconsistencies result in computed critical exponents that align with those of mean-field theory. We specify the Joule-Thomson expansion to define the isenthalpic heating and cooling regimes, so providing a comprehensive thermodynamic characterization of this non-singular spacetime.

</details>


### [124] [Balanced Metrics Know About SYZ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25733)
*Per Berglund,Tristan Hübsch,Vishnu Jejjala,Viktor Mirjanić,Challenger Mishra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的Donaldson算法变体，在环境空间的整体截面上而非流形本身上操作，从而在大度数k下仍能使用标准单项式基计算可解释的平衡度量。将该算法应用于Dwork族和完全交Calabi-Yau流形等多个族，发现度量参数在大型复结构极限附近服从新颖的幂律，并将其与SYZ猜想预言的Gromov-Hausdorff度量坍缩联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数值Ricci-flat度量虽越来越精确，但缺乏可解释性，难以提取理论洞见。为克服这一问题，需要一种能保持可解释性的算法。

Method: 提出基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的Donaldson算法变体，不在流形本身而在环境空间的整体截面上操作，从而在大度数k下仍可使用标准单项式基计算可解释的平衡度量。

Result: 在Dwork族和完全交Calabi-Yau流形等多个族中，发现度量参数在大型复结构极限（LCSL）附近服从新颖的幂律。

Conclusion: 将发现的幂律与SYZ猜想预言的Gromov-Hausdorff度量坍缩联系起来，为理解Calabi-Yau度量在退化极限下的行为提供了理论桥梁。

Abstract: Numerical Ricci-flat metrics on Calabi-Yau manifolds are becoming increasingly accurate. However, they often lack the interpretability required to extract theoretical insights. In this paper, we introduce a novel variant of Donaldson's algorithm based on the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse that operates on the global sections of the ambient space rather than the manifold itself. This approach allows us to use the canonical monomial basis to compute interpretable balanced metrics even at large degrees $k$. Applying our ambient algorithm to multiple families, including the Dwork family and complete intersection Calabi-Yau manifolds, we discover that the metric parameters obey novel power laws near the Large Complex Structure Limit (LCSL). We connect these to the Gromov-Hausdorff metric collapse predicted by the SYZ conjecture.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum Field Theory Of Cosmological Perturbations Induced By Ultralight Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27133)
*Tomislav Prokopec,Marco Vecchioni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立第一性原理场论框架，研究超轻标量场（ULDM）在物质主导时期对线性宇宙学扰动的影响，保留其量子本质。发现经典ULDM凝聚体对引力波传播无影响，但压缩态的量子压力导致的引力子有效质量时变可在特定原初引力波模式中驱动参数共振，然而对非相对论性ULDM在物质辐射平衡时这种增长可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物质主导时期原初扰动的增长主要由暗物质驱动，超轻标量场（ULDM）是重要候选者，但传统模型将其视为经典高占据数体系。本文旨在建立包含量子效应的场论框架，准确评估ULDM对宇宙学线性扰动的影响。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，推导引力子场动力学的闭式方程，在绝热近似（WKB）下计算并正则化高斯初态ULDM场的源项，采用中点工作假设处理非局域项，并进行规范固定。

Result: 经典ULDM凝聚体对引力波传播无影响；压缩态的量子压力造成引力子有效质量随时间变化，可在特定原初引力波模式中驱动参数共振；但该增长在非相对论ULDM（质量范围10^{-21}-10^{-24} eV）于物质-辐射平衡时期可忽略。

Conclusion: 通过场论方法修正了此前关于ULDM经典凝聚体影响引力波传播的观点，指出量子效应虽然能产生参数共振，但在实际感兴趣的质量范围内该效应不显著。

Abstract: The growth of primordial perturbations during the matter-dominated era is primarily driven by dark matter. Ultralight scalar fields (ULDM) are a promising candidate for this role, conventionally modeled as operating in a classical, high-occupation regime. In this work, we develop a first-principles field-theoretic framework to investigate the impact of ULDM on linear cosmological perturbations during matter domination, explicitly retaining its quantum nature. Deriving a closed equation for the graviton field dynamics, we compute and regularize its source terms for a generic Gaussian initial state of the ULDM field within the adiabatic (WKB) approximation, employing the middle-point working assumption for non-local terms. After gauge-fixing we find that, contrary to previous claims, the classical condensate of ULDM has no influence on gravitational wave propagation. However, the time-dependent graviton effective mass induced by quantum pressure of the squeezed state can drive parametric resonance in specific primordial gravitational wave modes. We demonstrate this growth is negligible for non-relativistic ULDM at matter-radiation equality under the assumption of a power-law squeezing spectrum for masses in the range $m \sim 10^{-21}{-}10^{-24}$ eV.

</details>


### [126] [Quadratic Axion Couplings in String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27190)
*Naman Agarwal,Andrew R. Frey,Ratul Mahanta,Evan McDonough*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出轴子与电磁场二次耦合项 θ²F² 在弦论中的产生机制（经典、微扰、非微扰），发现微扰和非微扰量子贡献（如瞬子）产生的耦合被抑制（g ≪ 1，单位 1/f²），但仍大于 QCD 轴子通过带电π介子圈产生的类似耦合。这表明二次耦合在弦论轴子景观中普遍存在，可作为探测弦论轴子候选者（如暗物质、暗能量）及轴子谱学的重要探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轴子搜索主要关注与光子的线性耦合 θF\tilde{F}，但二次耦合 θ²F² 具有新颖现象学且尚未被充分探索，需要从弦论角度论证其产生机制及普遍性。

Method: 在弦论框架下分类提出三种产生二次耦合的机制：经典（classical）、微扰（perturbative）和非微扰（non-perturbative），并通过基准例子（benchmark examples）计算耦合强度 g，与 QCD 轴子通过带电π介子圈产生的类比耦合进行比较。

Result: 微扰和非微扰量子贡献（如瞬子）产生的二次耦合被抑制，g ≪ 1（以 1/f² 为单位），但仍显著大于 QCD 轴子的类比耦合。这表明二次耦合在弦论轴子景观中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 二次轴子-规范场耦合在弦论中普遍存在，应被认真对待作为探测弦论轴子景观的探针，既用于弦论中动态轴子候选者（暗物质、暗能量）的搜索，也用于弦论轴子景观的谱学研究。

Abstract: Axions and axion-like particles are a compelling candidate for physics beyond the standard model. While many axion searches are focused on the linear coupling to photons $θF \tilde{F}$, the possibility of a quadratic coupling to the electromagnetic kinetic term, $θ^2 F^2$, leads to novel phenomenology and new opportunities for testing axion-like particles. In this work we propose mechanisms for generating this coupling in string theory, which can be broadly classified as classical, perturbative, and non-perturbative. In benchmark examples, we find that both perturbative and non-perturbative quantum contributions such as instantons lead to couplings that are suppressed, $g \ll 1$ in units of $1/f^2$ where $f$ is axion decay constant, though easily larger than analogous coupling of the QCD axion that is generated through loops of charged pions. These analyses suggest that quadratic axion couplings to gauge fields are ubiquitous in string theory, and should be taken seriously as a probe of the string theory axiverse, both of string theory candidates for dynamical axions, such as dark matter or dark energy, and for spectroscopy of the string theory axiverse.

</details>


### [127] [The gauge invariance of non-perturbative vertex prescriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27213)
*M. E. Carrington,A. R. Frey,B. A. Meggison*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维量子电动力学中两种非微扰方法（Schwinger-Dyson方程和3PI有效作用量）的规范不变性，发现大耦合下Ward恒等式存在显著违反，3PI方法略优于SD方法，且Ball-Chiu ansatz在小耦合下表现良好但在大耦合下偏差较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估非微扰方法在规范理论中的规范不变性程度，检验Ward恒等式的满足情况。

Method: 在朗道规范下，分别使用Schwinger-Dyson积分方程组和3粒子不可约有效作用量方法实现顶点，通过与Ward恒等式的符合程度来检验规范不变性。

Result: 大耦合下Ward恒等式存在显著违反；3PI方法略优于SD方法；Ball-Chiu ansatz在小耦合下与两种非微扰顶点吻合较好，大耦合下偏差增大。

Conclusion: 3PI有效理论在保持规范不变性方面略优于SD顶点方法，但两种方法在大耦合下均存在明显问题；Ball-Chiu ansatz的适用范围受耦合强度限制。

Abstract: We study the gauge invariance of different continuum methods to include non-perturbative effects in gauge theories. We work with three dimensional quantum electrodynamics and implement vertices using two different methods: a set of coupled Schwinger-Dyson (SD) integral equations, and the self-consistent equations obtained from the 3-particle irreducible (3PI) effective action. We work in Landau gauge and assess the extent to which results are gauge invariant by checking how well the Ward identity is satisfied. Our results show that there is a fairly significant violation of the Ward identity at large coupling, although the 3PI effective theory is slightly better than the SD vertex. We also compare the results of both calculations with the commonly used Ball-Chiu ansatz and show that the agreement of the ansatz with both non-perturbative vertices is fairly good at small coupling but deviates more significantly at large coupling. We compare results for the two point functions of the theory and discuss the possible implications for phase transitions.

</details>

Abstract: We constrain the Emergent Fractional Fractal (EFF) cosmological model through a joint likelihood analysis of recent cosmological observations at the background and perturbation levels. In this framework, an effective fractal dimension $d$ is introduced to parameterize possible fractional deviations from the standard cosmological model. We consider three combinations of datasets: (i) late-time (LT) observations including PantheonPlus Type Ia supernovae, $H(z)$ measurements, and growth-rate measurements $fσ_8$; (ii) LT combined with DESI DR2 BAO and Big Bang nucleosynthesis (BBN); and (iii) LT combined with DESI DR2 BAO and CMB distance priors. With the inclusion of CMB distance priors, the fractal dimension is constrained to $d = 2.0004^{+0.0006}_{-0.0003}$ at the $1σ$ confidence level. Model comparison using the Akaike Information Criterion (AIC) shows that the EFF and $Λ$CDM models fit the observational data equally well, while the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) favors the simpler $Λ$CDM model because of its smaller parameter space. These results show that current cosmological observations place strong constraints on fractal extensions of the standard cosmological framework and possible deviations from the $Λ$CDM model.

</details>

Abstract: Dark matter deficient galaxies provide a direct way to test whether baryons and dark matter can become sufficiently separated during galaxy evolution. We formulate a Dark Matter-Baryon Separability condition based on the relative incorporation efficiencies of the two components, requiring the final dark matter to baryon ratio to fall below an observationally defined threshold. Applied to high speed collisions, this condition constrains dark matter baryon momentum transfer cross section, interacting dark matter fraction and the efficiency of gravitational recapture. The same framework gives us bounds on the abundance and cooling time of dissipative dark matter, and on the integrated escape rate of ultralight fuzzy dark matter from shallow or tidally disturbed potentials. These results show how dark matter deficient galaxies can complement cosmological and laboratory probes by constraining late time dark matter interactions, dissipation and halo stability.

</details>


### [130] [Phantom-Divide Crossing in Barrow-Tsallis Holographic Dark Energy with a Scale-Dependent Barrow Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26089)

Conclusion: 熵偏离贝肯斯坦-霍金形式会导致丰富的晚期宇宙学行为，且热力学一致性对暗能量熵的存在提出了必要要求。

Abstract: We consider a spatially flat cosmological model containing a noninteracting barotropic fluid and holographic dark energy with the future event horizon as the infrared cutoff. We parametrize the deviation of the horizon entropy from the Bekenstein--Hawking form by a smooth positive function of the horizon radius.
  We reduce the background evolution to a closed autonomous system and analytically describe the crossing of the phantom divide, $w=-1$. For entropies growing more slowly than the fourth power of the radius, the crossing is unique, occurs at an extremum of the event-horizon radius, and proceeds from the quintessence regime into the phantom regime. The local Chevallier-Polarski-Linder coefficient $w_{a,\mathrm{loc}}$ at the crossing is then positive, and its sign directly constrains the local entropy-scaling dimension at the horizon scale.
  The crossing kinematics yields an exact expression for the local entropy-scaling dimension, and a local prescription for the scale-dependent Barrow exponent in the Barrow--Tsallis entropy enables an analytic reconstruction of the entropy function. The early-time asymptotics remain consistent with the standard matter- and radiation-dominated eras, while the event-horizon consistency criterion selects physically admissible late-time trajectories leading to de Sitter states, to Type III or Big Rip singularities, or, for $δ=1$, to accelerated power-law expansion. The analytic results are illustrated numerically. Within the adopted decomposition of the total entropy, the generalized second law of thermodynamics further requires an additional dark-energy entropy in the phantom regime: the equilibrium entropy evolution implied by the Gibbs relation fails to satisfy the law already at the crossing, where the purely barotropic adiabatic description of perturbations also breaks down.

</details>


### [131] [Early- and late-time constraints on Wald-Gauss-Bonnet topological dark energy and implications for the $H_0$ and $S_8$ tensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26101)

Method: 将WGB模型作为有效流体实现在修改版CLASS玻尔兹曼求解器中，利用Planck、ACT DR6、SPT-3G的CMB数据，DESI DR2的BAO数据以及Pantheon+超新星数据，进行早期和晚期宇宙联合贝叶斯参数估计和模型约束。

Result: 全数据集以~3σ显著度（Cn=0.435^{+0.150}_{-0.132}）偏好非零WGB贡献，表现为类幻影偏离；将H₀从68.5提升至69.8 km/s/Mpc，哈勃张力缓解约0.9σ；S₈轻微增加；原初CMB几乎不变，但透镜效应和小尺度成团性增强。

Conclusion: WGB拓扑暗能量是一个物理动机明确且在观测上可行的晚期暗能量机制，能够在无需引入新早期宇宙物理的情况下部分缓解哈勃张力，但伴随H₀-S₈之间的固有权衡。

Abstract: The persistent $H_0$ and $S_8$ tensions motivate the search for new dark-energy mechanisms capable of modifying the late-time expansion history while preserving the successful early-Universe predictions of $Λ$CDM scenario. Wald-Gauss-Bonnet (WGB) topological dark energy provides a physically motivated realization of this possibility, where the effective dark-energy sector emerges from cosmic horizon thermodynamics and the black-hole formation and merger history. We present the first early- and late-Universe analysis of WGB cosmology, implementing the model as an effective fluid in a modified \texttt{CLASS} solver and constraining it against CMB data from \textit{Planck}, ACT~DR6 and SPT-3G, DESI~DR2 BAO, and Pantheon+ supernovae. While late-time data alone are consistent with $Λ$CDM, the full dataset prefers a non-zero WGB contribution, $\Cn=0.435^{+0.150}_{-0.132}$, corresponding to a $\sim3σ$ phantom-like deviation and an improved fit. The preferred solution raises $H_0$ from $68.5$ to $69.8~\kmsmpc$, reducing the Hubble tension by $\sim0.9σ$, at the cost of a mild increase in $S_8$. The reconstructed cosmological observables show that WGB leaves the primary CMB almost unchanged while enhancing lensing and small-scale clustering, revealing a characteristic $H_0$-$S_8$ trade-off. WGB dark energy therefore emerges as a physically motivated and observationally viable late-time mechanism for partially alleviating the Hubble tension without introducing new early-Universe physics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究将退化真空虫洞作为球壳内部替代平坦时空时，Israel连接条件和Brown-York质量关系如何改变，以及这种改变对构型物理意义的启示。

Method: 采用Israel连接条件分析刚性球壳在史瓦西外部时空中的嵌入，将内部平坦时空替换为退化Klinkhamer虫洞几何，比较两种情形下球壳的固有质量公式。

Result: 在退化虫洞内部下，球壳的固有质量（由Brown-York关系给出）为零，而ADM质量和外部史瓦西几何保持不变，构型完全由虫洞几何承载引力场。

Conclusion: 这种构型中引力场完全由虫洞几何而非壳物质承载，与先前退化虫洞的坍塌动力学存在关联，对引力理论中质量与几何的关系具有深层含义。

Abstract: A static configuration consisting of a rigid spherical shell enclosing a degenerate Schwarzschild Klinkhamer wormhole is investigated in general relativity. The shell is analyzed using the Israel junction conditions, while the total ADM mass is assumed to remain fixed. For an ordinary rigid shell in Schwarzschild spacetime, the proper mass is given by the Brown York relation. It is shown that replacing the flat interior by a degenerate vacuum wormhole changes the junction conditions in such a way that the proper mass of the shell vanishes, whereas the ADM mass and the exterior Schwarzschild geometry remain unchanged. The resulting configuration is therefore entirely characterized by the wormhole geometry as the carrier of the gravitational field. The physical interpretation of this result is discussed, together with its relation to the previously established collapse dynamics of degenerate wormholes.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate an extended cosmological scenario based on the Barrow-Tsallis entropy, incorporating a varying (i.e., energy-scale-dependent) anomalous dimension. This behavior is reminiscent of quantum gravity and effective field theory settings, where the relevant couplings acquire a nontrivial scale dependence. By applying the gravity-thermodynamic conjecture on the apparent horizon of a flat Friedmann-Robertson-Walker Universe, we derive the corresponding modified cosmological equations. The standard dynamics is recovered as a limiting case when the Barrow-Tsallis entropy reduces to the conventional Bekenstein-Hawking form. The proposed model is tested against a combination of early- and late-time datasets, including observational Hubble parameter measurements, the Pantheon+ catalog of Type~Ia supernovae, the second data release of DESI baryon acoustic oscillations and cosmic microwave background constraints. Using the Bayesian Information Criterion, we finally compare the fitting performance of our framework with that of the $Λ$CDM paradigm. While the latter remains mildly favored, our model is shown to be fully compatible with current observations within suitable regions of the parameter space, unveiling a richer phenomenology that points towards a possible alleviation of the Hubble tension.

Abstract: We study electromagnetic wave propagation in metric--affine geometries, where torsion and non-metricity may be present and the coupling between electromagnetism and spacetime is no longer unique. Rather than choosing a particular coupling prescription a priori, we construct electromagnetic sectors that preserve standard $U(1)$ gauge invariance and projective invariance of the affine connection as guiding symmetry principles. We introduce two representative models, one in which the Maxwell term is dressed by a scalar prefactor built from non-Riemannian invariants, and another in which the kinetic term is modified by a rank--four constitutive tensor acting as an anisotropic medium. We derive their geometric--optics limits and show that their couplings can modify the effective light cone, change the relation between field amplitude and intensity, induce polarization-dependent propagation, and generate birefringence and mode mixing. These results thus provide the formal basis for a broader phenomenological study connecting torsion and non-metricity with electromagnetic observables in concrete metric--affine backgrounds, including black-hole imaging, birefringent lensing, polarization observables, and departures from photon-number conservation.

### [135] [Fermionic Backreaction on Quantum Spacetimes: Cosmological Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26318)
