<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在圈量子宇宙学中，费米子作为物质场在量子时空上的传播及其反作用效应尚未被系统理解，本文旨在建立一个统一的哈密顿框架来刻画这一相互作用。

Method: 采用哈密顿形式，将狄拉克费米子在闭FLRW背景上展开为自旋球谐函数，从而将场动力学约化为时间依赖的费米振子系统；在试探场近似下推导修饰度规，并在Born-Oppenheimer框架下处理费米子反作用。

Result: 有质量费米子同时探测时间和空间量子几何修正，无质量费米子仅受时间重参数化影响；费米子反作用通过两个不同通道产生模式依赖的彩虹度规；反作用可修改量子反弹并产生有效宇宙学常数。

Conclusion: 费米子在圈量子宇宙学中扮演独特角色，其反作用效应导致丰富的物理现象，为理解量子时空上的量子场论开辟了新方向。

Abstract: This article reviews a Hamiltonian framework for describing Dirac fermions propagating on quantum cosmological spacetimes within loop quantum cosmology. Expanding the fermionic field in spinor harmonics on a closed Friedmann--Lemaître--Robertson--Walker background reduces the dynamics to a collection of time-dependent Fermi oscillators, providing a Schrödinger-picture description of fermionic perturbations on a quantum geometry. We discuss the emergence of dressed metrics in the test-field approximation, showing that massive fermions probe both temporal and spatial quantum-geometry corrections, whereas massless fermions, owing to conformal invariance, are affected only through a reparametrization of time. We further review the incorporation of fermionic backreaction within a Born--Oppenheimer framework, where the finite-dimensional Hilbert space of each fermionic mode gives rise to two distinct backreaction channels that naturally generate mode-dependent dressed (rainbow) metrics. Finally, we discuss the cosmological implications of fermionic backreaction, including state-dependent modifications of the quantum bounce and the emergence of an effective cosmological constant in the semiclassical regime. These results highlight the distinctive role of fermionic matter in loop quantum cosmology and outline open directions for understanding quantum fields on quantum spacetimes.

</details>


### [136] [Dendro-GR at high mass ratios with high spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26359)
*William K. Black,David W. Neilsen,Eric W. Hirschmann,David F. Van Komen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文报道了Dendro-GR在降低高质比自旋双黑洞并合数值模拟计算成本方面的重要进展，成功模拟了质比q=24（无自旋）和q=12（自旋χ=0.8）的体系，生成了精确引力波形，为即将发射的LISA探测器所需的引力波分析奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA等第三代引力波探测器将每年探测到高质比旋进双黑洞并合事件，但现有数值相对论模拟在该参数区域（高质比、高自旋、进动取向）计算成本极高且技术难度大，导致参数空间采样不足，造成参数估计的显著偏差，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 使用Dendro-GR框架，对质比q=24（无自旋）和q=12（双黑洞自旋最高达χ=0.8、多种位形）的双黑洞系统进行数值演化，提取多极模至ℓ=8阶的引力波形。

Result: 成功运行了首批Dendro-GR高质比模拟，生成了精确引力波形，模拟保持稳定，约束违反低且基本恒定，视界质量守恒，计算效率高且墙钟时间成本较低。

Conclusion: 这些概念验证结果表明Dendro-GR能够有效模拟高质比、高自旋双黑洞系统，为系统探索该参数区域及LISA引力波分析建立了起点。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) launches in less than a decade; it will detect spinning high-mass-ratio binary black hole inspirals annually, alongside other third-generation gravitational wave detectors. High-mass-ratio systems occupy a regime where numerical-relativity simulations remain computationally expensive and technically demanding, especially with high spins at precessing orientations. This portion of parameter space thus remains undersampled, leading to significant bias in parameter estimation. We must close these gaps.
  Here we report key progress in Dendro-GR toward reducing the computational cost of high-mass-ratio binaries with spin. We evolve the first Dendro-GR binaries at mass ratio $q=24$ (nonspinning) and at $q=12$ with spins up to $χ=0.8$ on both black holes, spanning various configurations. These proof-of-concept runs show strong evidence that Dendro-GR can simulate in this regime and beyond. The simulations generate accurate gravitational waveforms through multipole modes up to $\ell=8$, remain stable, keep constraint violations low and largely constant, conserve horizon mass, and have high computational efficiency with relatively low wall-hour cost. These results establish our starting line for systematic exploration of the high-mass-ratio, high-spin binary black hole systems that are needed for gravitational wave analysis.

</details>


### [137] [Charged Dirac stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26374)
*Maribel Hernández Márquez,Miguel Alcubierre*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在3+1广义相对论框架下求解了耦合的爱因斯坦-狄拉克-麦克斯韦(EDM)系统，研究了单重态自旋态中两个费米子的静态球对称构型。通过数值打靶法，针对不同电荷参数q找到了狄拉克场的多种稳态自引力解，并分析了电荷对结合能、质量、半径和致密性的影响。研究发现引力束缚构型仅存在于q<m时，质量-频率关系呈现与玻色场类似的螺旋结构，部分构型的致密性可与中子星相当。此外还发现了q略大于m的超临界解，但这些解在引力上非束缚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自旋1/2费米子场与引力耦合时的自引力束缚构型的存在性与性质，并比较不同自旋(s=0,1/2,1)场在引力耦合下的共性与差异。

Method: 采用3+1广义相对论形式，求解耦合的爱因斯坦-狄拉克-麦克斯韦方程组，通过数值打靶法寻找静态球对称二费米子体系的稳态解，并扫描不同电荷参数q下的解族。

Result: 引力束缚解仅存在于q<m区域；质量-频率关系呈现螺旋结构；部分解致密性可与中子星相当；q>m时存在超临界解但均为引力非束缚态。

Conclusion: 经典层面上，不同自旋(s=0,1/2,1)的自引力场在耦合引力时表现出一些共同特征，如螺旋结构质量-频率关系；超临界解在引力非束缚意义上不构成稳定的束缚态。

Abstract: In this work we solve the coupled Einstein-Dirac-Maxwell (EDM) system for static spherically symmetric configurations of two fermions in a singlet spinor state within the $3+1$ formalism of general relativity. We find different families of stationary self-gravitating solutions for the Dirac field through a numerical shooting method for different values of the electric charge parameter q. Furthermore, we investigate the effect of the charge q on the binding energy, mass, radius, and compactness of the solutions. We show that gravitationally bound configurations exist only for $q<m$, with m the mass of the Dirac field, and that the mass frequency relation exhibits the characteristic spiral structure previously found for bosonic fields of spin $s=0$ and $s=1$. We are able to show that some of these gravitationally bound configurations have a compactness comparable to that of neutron stars. With these results, we conclude that at least at the classical level, self-gravitating fields with different spins $s=0,1/2,1$ share some common characteristics when they are coupled to gravity. As has been previously shown for the case of bosonic stars, we also find some super-critical solutions with q slightly larger than m. Such super-critical solutions correspond to configurations such that in the Newtonian regime the Coulomb repulsion overcomes the gravitational attraction, and as such they would not be expected to exist. Nevertheless, even if they do exist in the general relativistic case for a limited range of values of $q>m$, we find that they are always gravitationally unbound.

</details>


### [138] [Exact solutions for static spherically symmetric spacetime with a perfect fluid in Rastall theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26463)
*Yoshimune Tomikawa,Ichika Obara,Takumi Orimo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Rastall理论中扩展了广义相对论中静态球对称完美流体的Liouvillian解，给出了Rastall参数与状态方程参数w的关系，推导出除w=-1/6外与广义相对论对应的精确解，并发现当w≠1/3,-1时存在多种依赖于常数选择的解类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中已知静态球对称完美流体在特定状态方程参数(w=0,-1/6,-1/5,-1/3,-1)下才存在Liouvillian精确解，作者希望将这些解扩展到Rastall引力理论中，探索更丰富的解空间。

Method: 在Rastall理论框架下，考虑静态球对称度规和完美流体能量-动量张量，结合状态方程p(r)=wρ(r)，推导Rastall参数与w的关系，然后求解爱因斯坦-拉斯特尔场方程得到精确解。

Result: 得到了Rastall理论中与广义相对论对应的精确解（除w=-1/6外）；发现当w≠1/3,-1时存在多种类型的解，其行为依赖于常数的选择。

Conclusion: Rastall理论提供了比广义相对论更丰富的静态球对称完美流体解结构，且通过Rastall参数与w的关系保持了与广义相对论的部分对应性。

Abstract: In general relativity, exact Liouvillian solutions for a static and spherically symmetric spacetime with a perfect fluid and the equation of state $p(r)=wρ(r)$ are known only for $w=0,-\frac{1}{6}, -\frac{1}{5}, -\frac{1}{3}, -1$. We extend this setup to Rastall's theory, presenting the relation between the Rastall parameter and the constant $w$, and deriving exact solutions that correspond to the known counterparts in general relativity, except for $w=-\frac{1}{6}$. Furthermore, we find that, when $w\neq \frac{1}{3}, -1$, there exist several types of solutions whose behavior changes depending on the choice of constants.

</details>


### [139] [Chaotic Imprints in Gravitational Waves from Conformal-Anomaly-Corrected Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26493)
*Wei-Hao Zhang,Yu-Sen An*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子共形反常修正中心黑洞的极端质量比旋进(EMRI)系统中混沌轨道对引力波信号的影响。利用数值近似方法计算不同轨道的引力波波形、频率分布和能谱，发现轨道能量或反常系数的变化会导致轨道从规则运动转变为混沌运动，这种动力学转变在引力波信号上留下明显印记：混沌轨道产生的引力波具有不规则的振幅波动、丰富的精细谱峰和连续谱分布。通过与LISA、Taiji和TianQin等未来空间引力波探测器的灵敏度曲线对比，证明这些探测器能捕获共形反常修正的混沌系统引力波信号，为天文观测中探测共形反常修正提供了潜在途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子共形反常修正黑洞背景中混沌轨道对EMRI引力波信号的影响，并评估利用未来空间引力波探测器检测共形反常修正的可能性。

Method: 采用数值近似方法计算沿不同轨道运动的致密天体产生的引力波波形，推导对应的频率分布和能谱，并将计算的特征应变与LISA、Taiji、TianQin等探测器的灵敏度曲线进行对比。

Result: 轨道能量或反常系数的变化驱动轨道从规则可积运动转变为混沌运动；混沌轨道产生的引力波具有不规则振幅波动、丰富精细谱峰和连续谱分布，与规则轨道引力辐射显著不同；LISA、Taiji和TianQin能捕获该混沌系统的引力波信号。

Conclusion: 混沌轨道引力波信号可作为探测共形反常修正的潜在途径，未来空间引力波探测器有望从天文观测中检测到共形反常修正效应。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the effect of chaotic orbits on the extreme mass ratio inspiral (EMRI) gravitational wave signals where the central black hole is corrected by quantum conformal anomaly. We utilize the numerical kludge method to compute gravitational waveforms produced by the compact object along different orbital trajectories, and also derive the corresponding frequency distribution and energy spectra of gravitational waves. Our calculations reveal that variations in orbital energy or anomaly coefficient drive the orbital evolution from regular integrable motion to chaotic motion, and such dynamical transition leaves clear imprints on gravitational-wave signal. Specifically, gravitational waves originating from chaotic orbits feature pronounced irregular and time-varying amplitude fluctuations, accompanied by abundant fine spectral spikes and extended continuous spectral distributions in both frequency and energy domains, which differ drastically from the gravitational radiation generated by the regular non-chaotic orbits. Moreover, we evaluate the detectability by comparing the calculated characteristic strain of gravitational waves emitted by the compact object on different orbits with the sensitivity curves of future space-based GW detectors, including LISA, Taiji and TianQin. The results demonstrate that these detectors are capable of capturing gravitational-wave signals from chaotic systems modified by conformal anomalies, which provide a potential pathway for detecting conformal anomaly correction in astronomical observation.

</details>


### [140] [Constraining deviations from the Teukolsky equation with GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26561)
*Sebastian H. Völkel,Nicola Franchini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LVK合作组探测到的引力波事件GW250114，首次对超越Teukolsky形式论的理论无关偏差参数进行了约束。通过将最终质量与自旋的后验信息作为参数化先验，结合公开的阻尼正弦波后验数据，展示了如何有效约束超越Teukolsky势，结果与标准Teukolsky方程一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前引力波探测精度不断提高，需要对广义相对论进行更严格的检验。超越Teukolsky形式论提供了一个理论无关的框架来参数化偏离GR的效应，但尚无实际观测约束。本文旨在利用高信噪比事件GW250114首次给出这种偏差的观测界限。

Method: (1) 采用超越Teukolsky形式论，将扰动方程有效势中的偏差直接与准正则模(QNM)谱的变化相联系；(2) 从完整的LVK旋近-合并-振铃分析中提取最终质量与自旋的参数化先验；(3) 使用公开的LVK关于理论无关衰减正弦波参数的后验信息；(4) 利用高信噪比简化似然函数，仅针对基模QNM进行分析，避免完整贝叶斯计算。

Result: 当前对偏差参数的约束与Teukolsky方程（即广义相对论）一致，未发现显著偏离。该方法成功避免了昂贵的完整贝叶斯分析，为未来更高信噪比事件提供了快速检验超越GR理论的途径。

Conclusion: 超越Teukolsky形式论可以有效地对理论无关的GR偏离进行观测约束，且该方法在高信噪比下具有高效性，未来可用于更多引力波事件的快速检验。

Abstract: The recent gravitational-wave detection GW250114 by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) Collaboration provides unprecedented precision for testing general relativity (GR) through black hole ringdowns. In this study, we provide the first bounds on theory-agnostic deviations from the Teukolsky equation as described by the beyond-Teukolsky formalism. It directly connects deviations in the perturbation equations on the level of the effective potential in the Teukolsky equation with changes in the quasinormal mode (QNM) spectrum. We incorporate information on the final mass and spin from a full LVK inspiral-merger-ringdown analysis as parametrized priors in our analysis, reflecting theoretical uncertainties. Using publicly available LVK posterior information on agnostic damped sinusoid parameters, we then demonstrate how much beyond-Teukolsky potentials can be constrained. The high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) allows us to avoid the expensive full Bayesian analysis of all parameters and to work directly with a simplified likelihood for the fundamental QNM only. This strategy is promising for future events with even higher SNR and allows, in principle, for a quick and simple test of theories beyond GR without performing the full data analysis procedure. We report that current bounds on deviation parameters are in agreement with the Teukolsky equation.

</details>


### [141] [Gravitational bremsstrahlung waveform at the eighth post-Minkowskian order in the extreme-mass-ratio limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26614)
*Andrea Geralico*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在极端质量比极限下，计算了两个无自旋体散射产生的引力波波形，在频域中达到了第8后闵可夫斯基阶（$O(G^8)$，六圈）和分数第6后牛顿阶精度。此前$O(G^4)$的结果被补充了5PM辐射角动量和6PM辐射反应散射角。$O(G^4)$以下波形可用主积分表达，而从$O(G^5)$（四圈）开始，傅里叶积分结构变得复杂，出现了满足非齐次贝塞尔方程的新主积分族。该结果显著提高了散射波形的精度，此前仅知于一圈（量子振幅方法）或两圈（仅2PN精度，多极后闵可夫斯基形式）水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升极端质量比散射系统中引力波波形的计算精度，突破当前仅有一圈（量子振幅方法）或两圈（但仅2PN精度）水平，向更高阶后闵可夫斯基和后牛顿阶推进。

Method: 在极端质量比极限下，采用频域方法计算无自旋体散射引力波波形，通过主积分（双线性修正贝塞尔函数）和Meijer G函数表达低阶结果，对高阶则求解非齐次贝塞尔方程处理新主积分族。

Result: 实现了8PM（六圈）和6PN精度的散射波形计算；补充了5PM辐射角动量和6PM辐射反应散射角；揭示了从$O(G^5)$开始傅里叶积分结构复杂化，新主积分族满足以低阶主积分为源的非齐次贝塞尔方程。

Conclusion: 该工作显著提升了极端质量比散射波形的精度，是目前已知最高阶的后闵可夫斯基和后牛顿计算结果，为引力波天文学中极端质量比旋近系统的建模提供了重要理论基础。

Abstract: The gravitational waveform generated by the scattering of two nonspinning bodies is computed in the frequency domain in the extreme-mass-ratio limit at the eighth post-Minkowskian (PM) order (i.e., $O(G^8)$, or six-loop) and at the fractional sixth post-Newtonian (PN) order. Previous results at $O(G^4)$ are completed here by computing the 5PM radiated angular momentum as well as the 6PM radiation-reacted scattering angle. Up to that order the waveform is expressed in terms of few master integrals, with integrands bilinear in (modified) Bessel functions, leading to iterated Bessel functions which can be in turn expressed in terms of Meijer G functions. Starting from $O(G^5)$ (four-loop) the structure of Fourier integrals becomes quite involved. In fact, there are several new families of master integrals, which can be shown to satisfy inhomogeneous Bessel equations with master integrals of lower order as sources. Although limited to the first order in the mass ratio, the results presented here significantly improve the accuracy of the scattering waveform, currently known at the one-loop level from quantum-amplitude-based computations or at the two-loop level (but with 2PN accuracy only) by using the multipolar-post-Minkowskian formalism.

</details>


### [142] [Bayesian nonparametric estimation of correlated gravitational wave detector network noise using matrix-gamma process priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26619)
*Yixuan Liu,Renate Meyer,Nelson Christensen,Jeung Eun Lee,Jianan Liu,Patricio Maturana,Avi Vajpeyi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯非参数方法，直接估计引力波探测器（LISA和爱因斯坦望远镜）XYZ通道的噪声谱密度矩阵，利用矩阵-伽马过程先验保证埃尔米特正定性，并通过自适应MCMC进行后验采样，避免了可逆跳转方法。该方法能纠正错误指定的参数化噪声模型，仿真实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代引力波探测器（如LISA和ET）中，跨通道相关性必须被考虑，否则会导致引力波信号参数估计有偏。现有方法先在单链路层面分别估计测试质量和光学计量系统噪声，再通过已知传递函数映射到TDI通道，但无法容纳额外的不确定性来源。

Method: 提出贝叶斯非参数方法：1) 使用Bernstein多项式基展开表示谱密度矩阵；2) 引入矩阵-伽马过程先验，保证每个频率点上谱估计的埃尔米特正定性；3) 采用分块多元Whittle似然；4) 使用自适应MCMC算法进行后验采样，避免可逆跳转方法。

Result: 在LISA和ET的仿真研究及模拟相关噪声数据上的实验表明，该方法能有效估计噪声谱密度矩阵，并能纠正错误指定的参数化噪声模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯非参数框架能够直接、灵活地估计多通道噪声谱密度矩阵，适用于需要精确噪声模型以确保无偏参数估计的下一代引力波探测器数据分析。

Abstract: This paper addresses the important problem of estimating the noise spectral density of next-generation gravitational-wave detectors, such as LISA and the Einstein Telescope (ET), where cross-channel correlations must be accounted for to avoid biased parameter estimation of gravitational-wave signals. Unlike approaches that estimate test-mass and optical-metrology-system noise separately at the single-link level and then map them to the Time-Delay Interferometry (TDI) channels through known transfer functions, we develop a Bayesian nonparametric method that directly estimates the spectral density matrix of the XYZ channels, thereby accommodating additional sources of uncertainty. Our approach combines a flexible matrix-gamma process prior on the matrix-valued coefficients of a Bernstein polynomial basis expansion with a blocked multivariate Whittle likelihood. The prior guarantees Hermitian positive definiteness of the spectral estimate at every frequency. To avoid reversible-jump methods, we use an adaptive Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) algorithm for posterior sampling. The proposed framework can also be used to correct misspecified parametric noise models. Results from a simulation study and simulated correlated-noise data for both LISA and ET demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [143] [Generation and purification of excited spacetimes using Schwarzian derivative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26693)
*Rakesh K Jha,Akhil U Nair,Prasant Samantray,Sashideep Gutti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用 Schwarzian 导数的表达式建立微分方程，在二维弯曲时空量子场论框架下回答了三个基本问题：给定质量为零的标量场处于真空态的时空，找出所有具有热粒子分布的子区域（Unruh效应推广）；给定具有热粒子分布的时空，找出所有可能的“父”时空（纯化问题）；以及从同一父时空出发，找出所有具有相同粒子内容的“兄弟”时空。通过求解三阶非线性微分方程获得了这三类问题的通解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二维弯曲时空量子场论中，Unruh效应可通过CFT能动张量的反常变换律（含Schwarzian导数）推导。作者希望回答三个尚未被系统解决的基础问题：真空态时空中哪些子区域呈现热分布（Unruh效应的推广逆问题）、给定热态时空后哪些“父”时空能纯化它、以及哪些不同时空共享相同的粒子内容。

Method: 基于Schwarzian导数的表达式，分别建立三个三阶非线性微分方程。第一个方程用于求解真空态时空中所有具有热粒子分布的子区域；第二个方程求解给定热分布时空的所有可能父时空（包括仅纯化左移或右移扇区的“部分纯化”）；第三个方程求解从同一父时空出发具有相同粒子内容的兄弟时空。通过求解这些微分方程获得通解。

Result: 获得了第一个问题的通解，可以生成给定时空的各种具有热通量/粒子密度的子区域（Rindler时空是其中一个特例）。对于第二个问题，获得了一类通解，并定义了“部分纯化”概念。对于第三个问题，通过求解另一个基于Schwarzian导数的微分方程，获得了兄弟时空的通解。

Conclusion: Schwarzian导数提供了一个统一的数学框架来系统回答二维弯曲时空中与Unruh效应相关的三个基础问题，将热粒子分布、时空纯化与等粒子内容时空的分类问题转化为可求解的非线性微分方程。

Abstract: In this article, we use the expression of the Schwarzian derivative to set up differential equations to find answers to three fundamental questions in the context of QFT in curved spacetime, specifically in two dimensions. One of the ways in which one can derive the Unruh effect in two dimensions is to use the anomalous transformation law of the energy-momentum tensor for a CFT that involves a Schwarzian derivative (Virasoro Anomaly). We answer the following three questions. The first question is as follows: If we have a spacetime with a massless scalar field in vacuum, what are all the subsets of spacetime such that the subset has a thermal distribution of particles for the left-moving and/or right-moving sectors? We obtain a general solution to this question by setting up and solving a third-order nonlinear differential equation based on the expression of Schwarzian. Based on the general solution, we can generate various subsets of the given spacetime that have a thermal flux/density of particles, of which the Rindler spacetime is one. The second question is an inverse question in which we suppose we are given a spacetime with a thermal distribution of particles; what are the possible purifying spacetimes (the ``parent'' spacetimes with the field in vacuum state whose reduced state in the given spacetime yields the observed particle content)? We similarly obtain a general class of solutions by setting up and solving a second differential equation. In this context, we also define ``partial purification'' where we obtain a spacetime that purifies only the left-moving or right-moving sector. The third question concerns locating spacetimes with the same particle content starting from the same ``parent'' spacetime. These sibling spacetimes are generated again by obtaining the general solution of a third differential equation based on the expression of Schwarzian.

</details>


### [144] [A Bound on the Dynamical Love Number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26686)
*Alex Kehagias,Antonio Riotto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用因果性、实在性、被动性和高频条件，将致密天体的潮汐响应重新标度为一个上半复频率平面的全纯自映射，借助Schwarz-Pick定理给出了潮汐响应随频率变化率的边界：中子星的动力学Love数受静态Love数和第一内模频率约束，单模模型饱和该边界；黑洞则约束了耗散性潮汐加热系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为致密天体（中子星和黑洞）的潮汐形变响应提供一个基于第一性原理（因果性、实在性、被动性）的普适性理论边界，而不依赖于具体的微观物理模型。

Method: 将潮汐响应函数（推迟Green函数）通过重新标度转换为上半复频率平面的全纯自映射，然后应用Schwarz-Pick定理（此前用于推导黑洞物理中的量子混沌界限）来约束该映射的导数，从而得到潮汐响应随频率变化率的边界。

Result: 对于中子星，动力学Love数被静态Love数和第一内模（f-模）频率所限定，且单模模型恰好饱和该边界；对于黑洞，该边界给出了耗散性潮汐加热系数的信息。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个不依赖微观模型的普适性潮汐响应边界，为中子星和黑洞的潮汐形变研究提供了理论约束，并且单模模型在该框架下是最优的。

Abstract: The tidal deformability of a compact object is encoded in a single function of frequency, the retarded Green's function relating the induced multipole to the applied tide. Causality, reality, passivity, and the high-frequency conditions required for a positive-measure dispersion representation allow this response to be rescaled into a holomorphic self-map of the upper complex frequency half-plane. Using the Schwarz--Pick theorem, already employed to derive the quantum chaos bound in black hole physics, we provide a bound on the rate of the tidal response with respect to the frequency. For a neutron star the dynamical Love number is bounded in terms of the static one and of the frequency of the first internal mode, with the single-mode ($f$-mode) model saturating the bound. For a black hole, the bound gives information on the dissipative tidal-heating coefficient.

</details>


### [145] [Bouncing Cosmology and Cosmological Dynamics in $f(Q,T)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26753)
*Bhagwat Gidhad,A. S. Agrawal,S. A. Kadam*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(Q,T)引力框架下提出一个重构宇宙学模型，统一描述了早期和晚期宇宙演化。该模型展现非奇异性非对称反弹，平滑连接初始收缩相与后续膨胀宇宙，并自然演化到暗能量主导时代。研究聚焦于哈勃参数、能量密度、压强和态参数参量的演化，分析了宇宙演化的各阶段及暗能量特征。能量条件分析表明实现非奇异反弹的必要条件被违反。该重构模型有效捕捉了反弹周围的宇宙动力学，为早期和晚期宇宙演化提供了统一的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宇宙学模型难以同时统一描述早期反弹和晚期暗能量主导的宇宙演化，现有的f(Q,T)引力模型尚未能实现这种统一描述，因此需要构建一个能同时涵盖早期非奇异反弹和晚期暗能量加速膨胀的重构宇宙学模型。

Method: 在f(Q,T)引力理论框架下进行模型重构，分析哈勃参数、能量密度、压强和态参数等宇宙学量的演化行为，并通过能量条件分析来检验非奇异反弹的实现条件。

Result: 该模型成功展现了非奇异性非对称反弹，连接了初始收缩相和后续膨胀相，并自然过渡到暗能量主导的晚期时期。但能量条件分析表明，实现非奇异反弹所必需的能量条件被违反。

Conclusion: 重构后的f(Q,T)引力模型有效捕捉了反弹周围的宇宙动力学，为早期和晚期宇宙演化提供了统一且可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: We propose a reconstructed cosmological model in the framework of $f(Q,T)$ gravity, that provides a unified description of the early- and late-time evolution of the Universe. The model exhibits a non-singular asymmetric bounce, smoothly connecting an initial contracting phase to the subsequent expanding Universe and naturally evolving into a late-time dark energy-dominated epoch. Our study focuses on the progression of the Hubble parameter, energy density, pressure, and the parameter. This analysis aims to define the various stages of cosmic evolution and explore the characteristics of dark energy. The analysis of energy conditions reveals that the essential conditions for achieving a non-singular bounce are violated. Overall, the $f(Q, T)$ gravity model, once reconstructed, effectively captures the cosmic dynamics surrounding the bounce. It offers a cohesive theoretical framework that reliably explains the Universe's evolution during both its early and late stages.

</details>


### [146] [Bridging Superfluid and Nonminimally Coupled BEC Dark Matter through RAQUAL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26841)
*Stefano Liberati,Valentin Pomakov,Samuele Silveravalle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了超流体暗物质（SFDM）与玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚暗物质（BECDM）之间的关系。SFDM的MONDian表述是RAQUAL理论的牛顿低加速极限，其声子-重子相互作用映射为BECDM的导数耦合到爱因斯坦张量，加上对Ricci标量的非最小耦合。两者相互作用部分等价（仅相差爱因斯坦-希尔伯特项的线性扰动），但动力学部分不等价：BECDM的标准二次动能项无法映射为SFDM要求的非解析动能项。因此两个模型相关但非动力学等效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两者都受凝聚态物理启发，且都旨在调和星系尺度的MOND现象与更大尺度的粒子暗物质，因此需要厘清SFDM与BECDM之间的理论关系。

Method: 首先证明SFDM的MONDian表述是RAQUAL理论在爱因斯坦标架的牛顿低加速极限；然后将其协变相互作用部分变换到Jordan标架，与BECDM进行比较分析。

Result: SFDM的声子-重子相互作用映射为BECDM的导数耦合到爱因斯坦张量加上对Ricci标量的非最小耦合，相互作用部分等价（仅差爱因斯坦-希尔伯特项的线性扰动）；但动力学部分不等价，标准二次动能项无法映射为非解析动能项。

Conclusion: 两个模型相关但非动力学等效，该映射为SFDM相互作用提供了协变解释，并明确了两个框架之间可转移的理论性质。

Abstract: Motivated by their common condensed-matter inspiration and their shared aim of reconciling MOND-like phenomenology on galactic scales with particle dark matter on larger scales, we investigate the relation between Superfluid Dark Matter (SFDM) and Bose--Einstein Condensate Dark Matter (BECDM). Since SFDM is formulated in the Newtonian regime whereas BECDM is fully relativistic, we first show that the MONDian formulation of SFDM arises as the Newtonian, low-acceleration limit of a Relativistic AQUAdratic Lagrangian (RAQUAL) theory in the Einstein frame. We then transform its covariant interaction sector to the Jordan frame and compare it with BECDM. The phonon--baryon interaction of SFDM maps onto the BECDM derivative coupling to the Einstein tensor, supplemented by a small non-minimal coupling to the Ricci scalar. The interaction sectors are therefore equivalent up to a linear perturbation of the Einstein--Hilbert term. Their kinetic sectors, however, remain inequivalent: the standard quadratic kinetic term of BECDM cannot be mapped onto the non-analytic kinetic term required by SFDM. The two models are consequently related but not dynamically equivalent. This mapping provides a covariant interpretation of the SFDM interaction and clarifies which theoretical properties can be transferred between the two frameworks.

</details>

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Advanced Virgo在第四次观测运行中完成了信号回收镜的安装，实现了设计光学配置，以68.9%的占空比运行，对双中子星合并的中位探测距离达到53 Mpc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成Advanced Virgo的设计光学配置（安装信号回收镜），参与第四次引力波探测器联合观测运行，提升探测灵敏度。

Method: 在第三次和第四次观测运行之间进行升级，安装信号回收镜并调试该光学配置，同时与其他探测器（Advanced LIGO、KAGRA）协同观测。

Result: Advanced Virgo以68.9%的占空比运行，对双中子星合并的角度平均中位探测距离达到53 Mpc。

Conclusion: Advanced Virgo成功完成了设计光学配置，在第四次观测运行中实现了稳定的高性能运行。

Abstract: From April 10, 2024 to November 18, 2025 Advanced Virgo participated in the fourth observing run of the network of gravitational-wave detectors, together with Advanced LIGO and KAGRA. For this observing run Advanced Virgo has completed its design optical configuration with the installation of a signal recycling mirror. In this paper we describe the challenges encountered in commissioning this optical configuration, alongside the other upgrades performed between the third and fourth observing run. The Virgo detector operated with a 68.9% duty cycle and with an angle-averaged median range to binary neutron star mergers of 53 Mpc.

</details>


### [148] [Junction Conditions, Radial Stability, Thermodynamics, Optical Geometry and Appearance of Polymer-Quintessence Thin-Shell Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26882)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel,M. C. Araújo,J. Furtado,Celio R. Muniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了从聚合物黑洞（包含Kiselev-type quintessence）构造的具有非面积角函数的反射对称薄壳虫洞，通过Israel junction条件、稳定性分析、热力学分析和光学分析，揭示了聚合物修正和quintessence环境如何共同影响虫洞的物质含量、径向响应、热力学记账和光学外观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄壳虫洞对有效黑洞几何中的lapse函数和面积半径选择敏感，本文旨在探索在非面积角函数框架下，聚合物黑洞与quintessence环境共同作用对虫洞结构的影响。

Method: 从正lapse扇区的聚合物黑洞（被Kiselev-type quintessence包围）构造反射对称薄壳虫洞，保留非面积角函数贯穿连接、稳定性、热力学和光学分析全过程；使用Israel junction条件分析表面物质，将径向动力学写为有效势问题，并用线性barotropic、variable phantomlike和variable Chaplygin gas三种状态方程测试稳定性。

Result: 静态喉部表面能量密度为负；切向零和内在强能量组合由局部lapse斜率控制；非面积扇区产生动量通量项并修正局部稳定性判据；线性barotropic和variable phantomlike状态方程局部不稳定，variable Chaplygin gas存在有限线性径向稳定窗口；几何修正改变局部第一定律平衡和壳熵记账；光学分析显示跨喉传播产生额外内像分支。

Conclusion: 聚合物修正和quintessence环境共同重组了薄壳虫洞的物质内容、径向响应、热力学记账和光学外观，尽管虫洞与黑洞几何共享相同的外部临界曲线，但虫洞的光学图像会出现额外的内部像分支。

Abstract: Thin-shell wormholes built from effective black hole geometries are sensitive not only to the lapse function but also to the choice of areal radius. We construct a reflection-symmetric thin-shell wormhole from the positive-lapse sector of a polymer black hole surrounded by Kiselev-type quintessence and keep the nonareal angular function throughout the junction, stability, thermodynamic, and optical analyses. The Israel junction conditions give a negative surface energy density for every static throat on the positive branch, while the tangential null and intrinsic strong energy combinations are controlled by the local lapse slope. The radial dynamics is written as an effective-potential problem in which the nonareal sector produces a momentum-flux term and modifies the local stability criterion for surface equations of state with explicit radius dependence. For the sampled calibrated configurations, the linear barotropic and variable phantomlike closures remain locally unstable, whereas the variable Chaplygin gas admits finite linear radial stability windows. The same geometric correction also modifies the local first-law balance and shell entropy bookkeeping, while the optical analysis shows that cross-throat propagation generates additional inner image branches despite the wormhole and black hole geometries sharing the same exterior critical curve. These results identify how polymer corrections and a quintessence environment jointly reorganize the matter content, radial response, thermodynamic bookkeeping, and optical appearance of the resulting thin-shell wormhole.

</details>


### [149] [On a cosmological Oppenheimer-Snyder model: matching McVittie and FLRW spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26996)
*Brien C. Nolan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了膨胀的McVittie时空与空间平坦FLRW时空在一般超曲面（具有任意因果性质）上的光滑匹配条件，证明了匹配条件等价于一个三维非线性一阶常微分方程组，并证明了未来全局唯一解的存在性。研究发现匹配超曲面通常同时具有类空和类时部分，因此各向同性区域不能被视为McVittie外部区域的内部，即McVittie时空的各向同性源在全局意义上不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究McVittie宇宙黑洞时空是否存在各向同性的物质源，探讨其与Oppenheimer-Snyder模型的区别。

Method: 通过分析一般超曲面（因果性质可逐点变化）上的光滑匹配条件，将完整匹配条件简化为一个三维非线性一阶ODE系统（带单个初值约束），并利用半全局存在性定理进行数学分析。

Result: 匹配超曲面在未来的解全局唯一存在，在过去可延伸到McVittie区域的奇点；匹配超曲面通常同时具有类空和类时部分；各向同性区域不能作为McVittie外部区域的内部。

Conclusion: McVittie时空的各向同性源在全局意义上不存在，这与Oppenheimer-Snyder模型中各向同性尘埃球可通过全类时边界匹配到Schwarzschild外部的情况形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: We consider the necessary and sufficient conditions for the smooth matching of an expanding McVittie spacetime and a spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) spacetime across a general hypersurface - that is, a hypersurface of arbitrary causal character, where the character possibly changes from point to point. We identify a number of special cases, and determine some no-go results. In the general case, we show that the full set of matching conditions is equivalent to a 3-dimensional non-linear system of first order ODEs, subject to a single initial value constraint. Taking the perspective that the McVittie region is specified, we prove a semi-global existence result for this system and interpret these solutions in terms of the physical set-up. Solutions exist and are unique globally to the future. In this direction, the required FLRW spacetime exists and is unique and the matching hypersurface exists almost everywhere and is unique. To the past, the matching hypersurface extends to meet the past singularity of the McVittie region. We determine the causal character of the matching hypersurface in these limits, and consider the implications for the existence (or otherwise) of an isotropic source of the McVittie cosmological black hole spacetime. We find that generically, the matching hypersurface has spacelike as well as timelike portions, and so the isotropic region cannot be considered to be an interior for the McVittie exterior. This is in distinction to the Oppenheimer-Snyder model, in which an isotropic dust sphere matches across an everywhere-timelike boundary to the Schwarzschild exterior. On the basis of the sometimes-spacelike nature of the hypersurface, we conclude that an isotropic source of the McVittie spacetime does not exist in a global sense.

</details>


### [150] [Resumming Kerr Quasinormal-Mode Frequencies: Accuracy and Breakdown Near Extremality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27043)
*Jierui Hu,Kent Yagi,Nicolas Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用Padé和Borel-Padé重求和技术改进Kerr黑洞准正态模的高阶WKB展开的收敛性，在慢旋转和高自旋情况下分别进行了21阶和41阶WKB展开，但发现该方法在极端极限附近因势能近极点结构而失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr黑洞准正态模通常用数值方法计算，但解析近似有助于识别谱的物理机制。本文旨在探究能否通过Padé和Borel-Padé重求和使发散的WKB展开具有预测能力。

Method: 开发了两种互补实现：一是慢旋转展开（自旋a的21阶WKB半解析展开），二是固定自旋的Padé-WKB实现（41阶WKB阶频率方程重求和）。通过定点迭代与Leaver方法比较验证精度。

Result: 慢旋转下21阶重求和精度显著优于此前4阶近似。高自旋下，基模m=0在a=0.99时实部误差低于10^{-7}。但该方法在极端极限下对趋近零阻尼分支的模式失效。

Conclusion: 重求和WKB在大部分参数空间有效，但极端极限附近失效的原因在于Chandrasekhar-Detweiler势能的近视界结构：极点导致喉道尺度上的快速变化，局域泰勒展开无法均匀覆盖相关区域。

Abstract: Kerr black-hole quasinormal modes are usually computed with numerical methods, but analytic approximations remain useful for identifying the physics that controls different parts of the spectrum. In this paper, we ask whether the divergent, high-order Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin (WKB) expansion about the peak of the Chandrasekhar-Detweiler potential can be made predictive through Padé and Borel-Padé resummation. We develop two complementary implementations: a semi-analytic slow-rotation expansion in the dimensionless spin $a$ (carried out through 21th WKB order), and a fixed-spin Padé-WKB implementation for the resummed frequency equation (carried out through 41st WKB order). In the slow-rotation regime, the 21th-order resummed expansion is significantly more accurate than the fourth-order approximation found previously. For damped modes at larger spins, the fixed-point iteration agrees well with Leaver's method, reaching fractional errors below $10^{-7}$ in the real part of the fundamental $m=0$ mode at $a=0.99$. The same strategy fails for modes that approach the zero-damped branch near extremality. We trace this breakdown to the near-horizon structure of the Chandrasekhar-Detweiler potential. As the extremal limit is approached, nearby poles produce rapid variation on the throat scale, so a local Taylor expansion about the potential peak no longer uniformly captures the relevant region.

</details>


### [151] [Perturbing Gravitational Atoms: Negative Love, Resonant Tides and Shifted Resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27174)
*Mateja Bošković,Nikola Savić*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 worldline 有效场论框架下，系统研究了旋转黑洞超辐射不稳定性形成的引力原子的潮汐响应（Love 数），首次计算了自旋云的 Love 数，发现某些自旋态具有负的静态 Love 数且量级显著增强（约 10²–10³ 倍），并分析了双星早期旋近中的移位共振及竞争性扰动对可观测效应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转黑洞的超辐射不稳定性可形成引力原子，为探测超出传统探测策略的标量、轴子和矢量粒子参数空间提供了重要途径。然而，这类系统在双星中的动力学建模因不同扰动的竞争性质而变得微妙，亟需一个鲁棒的理论方案来处理这些扰动。

Method: 在 worldline 有效场论框架下，构建了一套处理引力原子内部扰动（相对论修正、自引力）和外部潮汐场的鲁棒方案；在此基础上计算了引力原子的 Love 数，包括自旋云的情况；并分析了双星早期旋近中的移位共振。

Result: 首次计算了自旋云的 Love 数，发现某些自旋态具有负的静态 Love 数，其量级相对于非自旋态有参量增强（在现象学相关场景中因子约 10²–10³）。同时，通过动力学图像识别了永久多极矩和诱导多极矩在双星演化的不同阶段分别主导可观测信号。

Conclusion: 引力原子是研究引力波物理中潮汐响应理论方面的有用 toy model；本文的结果为通过双星观测探测新轻玻色子提供了更完整的理论依据，尤其在移位共振和有限尺寸效应方面给出了清晰的动力学图像。

Abstract: The superradiant instability of rotating black holes can generate a significant overdensity of bosonic matter around them, together forming a gravitational atom. This mechanism allows one to probe a large part of the parameter space of scalars, axions and vectors that lies beyond the reach of traditional detection strategies. Modelling the dynamics of these systems in binaries is, however, subtle, due to the competing nature of the different perturbations. In this work, we provide a robust scheme to treat these perturbations, including both the minimal set of internal ones (relativistic corrections and self-gravity) and the external tidal field. Within the worldline effective field theory framework, we then calculate the Love numbers of gravitational atoms, for the first time also for the most interesting case of spinning clouds. Certain spinning states are found to have \textit{negative} (static) Love numbers, with a magnitude parametrically enhanced relative to the scaling for the non-spinning states -- in phenomenologically relevant scenarios by a factor $\mathcal{O}(10^2\text{--}10^3)$. Finally, we consider the binary evolution in the early inspiral, assessing the impact of the competing perturbations on \textit{shifted resonances}. This dynamical picture allows us to identify at which stages the permanent multipoles are the strongest indicators of new light bosons, and at which the induced ones take over, while keeping track of both types of finite-size effects even during the resonance. More broadly, our results demonstrate that gravitational atoms are a useful toy model for studying the theoretical aspects of tidal response in gravitational-wave physics.

</details>


### [152] [Discrete symmetries of modified Teukolsky equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27053)
*Ciro De Simone*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Teukolsky方程的离散对称性如何约束黑洞扰动及其准正则模谱，证明通过修改Teukolsky势能可以打破m=0简并性，并展示了离散对称性可用于更高效地刻画时域演化中的m=0准正则模。作为高阶引力理论的应用案例，揭示了频域势能的时域实现可能产生非物理的额外模式分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Teukolsky方程的离散对称性对黑洞扰动和准正则模谱具有重要约束作用，但其对称性破缺的效应以及这些对称性如何被用于更高效的模式表征尚待深入研究。

Method: 通过修改Teukolsky势能改变方程对称性结构，分别在频域（主Teukolsky方程）和时域（(2+1)维模拟）中证明结果，并利用离散对称性更高效地表征时域演化中的m=0准正则模。

Result: 修改Teukolsky势能可打破m=0准正则模的简并性；离散对称性可用于更高效地提取时域演化中的m=0准正则模；高阶引力理论中频域势能的时域实现会产生非物理的额外模式分支。

Conclusion: Teukolsky方程的离散对称性在黑洞微扰理论中具有重要应用价值，既可解释模式简并性破缺，又可提升计算效率，但需警惕时域实现中可能引入的非物理模式。

Abstract: The Teukolsky equation possesses discrete symmetries that constrain the properties of black hole perturbations and their quasinormal mode spectrum. In this study, we explore how a class of modifications of the Teukolsky potential can alter the symmetry structure of the equation and break the m = 0 degeneracy of quasinormal modes. We prove this result in frequency domain using the master Teukolsky equation and in time domain via (2+1)-dimensional simulations. We also show that the discrete symmetries can be leveraged for a more efficient characterization of the m = 0 quasinormal modes from time-domain evolutions. As a theory-specific application, we consider the case of higher-derivative theories of gravity, highlighting that time-domain implementations of frequency-domain potentials can give rise to additional non-physical branches of modes.

</details>


### [153] [Quadrupolar tidal effects destroy the integrability of black hole geodesics: analytic proof and numerical evidence of chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27129)
*Paul Ramond*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，在Kerr时空中，带有潮汐诱导四极矩的非自转天体无法保持Carter常数的守恒，因此潮汐扰动的测地线运动是混沌的、不可积的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际致密天体（如中子星）具有内部结构，会与背景时空曲率耦合。理解这种耦合对引力波源建模至关重要，而传统测试粒子近似忽略了这一效应。

Method: 通过协变哈密顿量形式将潮汐动力学定义在测地线相空间上，利用Kerr时空的代数和Killing对称性推导曲率潮汐标量与Carter常数的新关系，并结合数值诊断（庞加莱截面、Lyapunov指数、逃逸时间图）进行验证。

Result: 对于一般的潮汐耦合和非极端Kerr自旋，Carter常数不再守恒，潮汐扰动弹道出现随机层、对初值敏感性和分形盆地边界等混沌特征。

Conclusion: 主导阶潮汐动力学本质上是不可积的，这意味着传统可积框架下对不对称质量比旋近的建模需要修正。

Abstract: In general relativity, the motion of a test mass around a rotating black hole is described by Kerr geodesics. Owing to the symmetries of the Kerr spacetime, these geodesics possess four constants of motion, rendering the associated Hamiltonian system integrable. This integrability underlies much of the analytical framework used to model asymmetric-mass-ratio inspirals, key sources for future gravitational-wave detectors. Real compact bodies, however, are not test masses: their internal structure couples to the background curvature. In this work, we show that a non-spinning body endowed with a tidally induced quadrupole admits no deformation of the geodesic Carter constant that remains conserved, for generic tidal couplings and generic Kerr spin. Consequently, the leading-order tidal dynamics is generically non-integrable. The proof is analytic and relies on two key ingredients: a covariant Hamiltonian formulation of tidal dynamics on the same phase space as the geodesic problem, valid in arbitrary background spacetimes, and a novel relation between curvature tidal scalars and the geodesic Carter constant derived from the algebraic and Killing symmetries of Kerr spacetime. We complement this result with numerical diagnostics of the tidally perturbed dynamics, including Poincaré sections, Lyapunov exponents, and escape-time maps. These reveal chaotic structures in phase space, such as stochastic layers, sensitivity to initial conditions, and fractal basin boundaries, consistently with the analytic non-integrability result.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [A Riemann-Roch theorem for Frobenius quotients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26176)
*Matthew Dupraz,Andreas Gross,Leonid Monin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了两类交换Frobenius环——离散型K_f和连续型A_g，分别作为移位算子代数与微分算子代数关于多项式零化子的商环，用以模拟光滑完全簇的数值K-环和Chow环。当多项式满足Hirzebruch-Riemann-Roch定理的组合类比时，存在自然同构QK_f ≅ QA_g（扮演Chern特征角色），且K_f具有λ-环结构、Chern类、行列式及Serre对偶等丰富结构。本文还证明了函子性、Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch定理的组合类比，并通过截断Chern特征给出了积分同构K_f ≅ A_g。应用包括：计算拟阵的Snapper多项式的新公式、对偶类与拟阵及线性多面体族的对偶类之关联、环面簇丛的K-环实现等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为光滑完全簇的数值K-环和Chow环建立纯代数的组合模型，从而在非几何环境中再现Riemann-Roch理论的核心结构。

Method: 通过移位算子代数与微分算子代数关于多项式零化子的商构造两类Frobenius环K_f和A_g；定义Chern特征同构的条件（Hirzebruch-Riemann-Roch定理的组合类比）；引入Todd类作为连接多项式f和g的桥梁；构造截断Chern特征以实现积分同构。

Result: (1) 当f = td·g（td ∈ QA_g^×）时，存在自然同构QK_f ≅ QA_g；(2) K_f具有λ-环、Chern类、行列式、Serre对偶等结构；(3) 构造满足Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch定理的函子；(4) 截断Chern特征给出积分同构K_f ≅ A_g；(5) 应用：拟阵Snapper多项式新公式、对偶类一致性、环面簇丛K-环实现、Ehrhart扇K-环研究等。

Conclusion: 本文建立的Frobenius环框架统一了组合与几何中的Riemann-Roch理论，提供了计算拟阵Snapper多项式的有效工具，并揭示了环面簇丛K-环与Ehrhart扇K-环等对象的代数本质。

Abstract: We construct two families of commutative Frobenius rings: discrete and continuous Frobenius quotients $K_f$, resp. $A_g$, which are defined as the quotients of shift, resp. differential operator algebras by the annihilator of a polynomial. These constructions model the numerical $K$-rings and Chow rings of smooth complete varieties. We show that there is a naturally defined isomorphism $\mathbb{Q} K_f \cong \mathbb{Q} A_g$ playing the role of the Chern character, precisely when the polynomials satisfy a combinatorial analogue of the Hirzebruch-Riemann-Roch theorem, which states that there exists an invertible element $\mathrm{td} \in \mathbb{Q} A_g^\times$, called the Todd class, such that $f = \mathrm{td} \cdot g$. In this case we show that $K_f$ carries all the structure needed to make it a suitable model for $K$-rings of complete smooth varieties: $K_f$ is a $λ$-ring, has well-defined Chern classes, determinants, and satisfies a combinatorial analogue of Serre duality. We further show that the construction is functorial and obtain a combinatorial analogue of the Grothendieck-Riemann-Roch theorem. Lastly, we investigate the existence of larger families of isomorphisms between Frobenius quotients defined by the action of a power series on a generating set, such as the truncated Chern character which yields an integral isomorphism $K_f \cong A_g$.
  We provide numerous examples and applications: we give a new formula for computing Snapper polynomials of matroids, show that the dualizing class of $K_f$ coincides with dualizing classes of matroids and linear families of polytopes, realize $K$-rings of toric variety bundles as Frobenius quotients, and study $K$-rings of Ehrhart fans as well as exceptional isomorphisms in this setting.

</details>


### [155] [Algebraic vs. analytic: a survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26190)
*Aravind Asok,Tom Bachmann,Michael J. Hopkins*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究复代数簇之间的解析映射是否具有代数代表元的问题，特别关注非紧致情形，该问题与霍奇型猜想密切相关。具体探讨了两个核心问题：仿射簇上的全纯向量丛何时具有代数结构，以及同伦类何时具有代数代表元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究解析对象（解析映射、全纯向量丛、同伦类）是否能在代数几何框架下找到对应的代数代表元，这是连接解析几何与代数几何的核心问题，尤其在非紧致情形下与霍奇猜想等深刻猜想相关联。

Method: 从多个视角（代数几何、解析几何、霍奇理论）综合讨论该问题，通过具体实例（仿射簇上的全纯向量丛的代数化、映射同伦类的代数代表元）来阐释问题的具体表现形式。

Result: 指出非紧致代数簇情形下解析映射的代数代表元问题与霍奇型猜想紧密相关；全纯向量丛的代数结构问题与同伦类的代数代表元问题构成了该问题的具体体现。

Conclusion: 非紧致复代数簇之间解析映射的代数代表性问题是一个深刻的问题，其解决依赖于对霍奇型猜想的理解，并通过全纯向量丛和同伦类这两个具体问题给出了研究切入点。

Abstract: Given a pair of complex algebraic varieties, we can ask: does every analytic map admit an algebraic representative? Such questions are most interesting when the source and target are non-compact varieties, in which case the problems are closely linked with Hodge-type conjectures. Concrete avatars of this kind of problem, which we discuss from several points of view, include: which holomorphic vector bundles on an affine variety admit an algebraic structure, and which homotopy classes of maps admit algebraic representatives?

</details>


### [156] [Higher dimensional dominoes in de Rham-Witt cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26323)
*Yuanning Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文分类了二维de Rham-Witt上同调中的"domino"结构，将其化为标准型，并建立了与单势群、斜率谱序列微分、Brauer群指数等的关系，回答了Grammatica-Skorobogatov-Yang的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: de Rham-Witt上同调中的domino结构携带斜率谱序列的非零微分，但在一维之外尚未被分类，其性质不明。本文旨在填补这一空白，并回答Grammatica-Skorobogatov-Yang关于p-主Brauer群指数的相关问题。

Method: 通过为任意维数的domino建立标准型，并将其与两个单势群（一个形式群，一个完美群）相关联，证明它们的同源划分一致。在二维情形下，从Mazur-Ogus簇的晶体上同调中恢复domino，并计算超奇异阿贝尔簇的domino。

Result: 成功分类了二维domino，给出了任意维domino的标准型。证明了与domino关联的两个单势群的同源划分一致。对于两个超奇异阿贝尔簇族计算了domino，并用a-数给出了p-主Brauer群指数的上界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了domino结构的系统理论，回答了Grammatica-Skorobogatov-Yang的问题，为理解特征p光滑簇的de Rham-Witt上同调结构提供了新工具。

Abstract: The de Rham-Witt cohomology of a smooth proper variety in characteristic $p$ contains a canonical piece called the domino, which is not finitely generated over the Witt vectors and carries the nonzero differentials of the slope spectral sequence. Beyond dimension one, dominoes were unclassified. We classify the two-dimensional ones and put a domino of any dimension into a normal form. To each domino we attach two unipotent groups, one formal and one perfect, and prove that their isogeny partitions agree. In degree two we recover the domino of a Mazur-Ogus variety from its crystalline cohomology, compute it for two families of supersingular abelian varieties, and bound the exponent of the $p$-primary Brauer group in terms of the $a$-number, for every prime $p$. This answers a question of Grammatica-Skorobogatov-Yang.

</details>


### [157] [Twelve common flex lines in a general pencil of cubics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26396)
*Jihao Liu,Yanze Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了复数域上一般平面三次曲线束恰好有12条公共拐点线，回答了Ciliberto、Miranda和Roé提出的一个问题。该结果通过生成式AI（特别是ChatGPT 5.5 Pro和Danus系统）获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Ciliberto、Miranda和Roé提出的关于平面三次曲线束公共拐点线数量的问题。

Method: 利用生成式AI（ChatGPT 5.5 Pro和Danus系统）辅助证明。

Result: 一般平面三次曲线束恰好有12条公共拐点线。

Conclusion: 一般平面三次曲线束有12条公共拐点线，且该结果借助AI工具获得。

Abstract: We prove that a general pencil of plane cubics over $\mathbb C$ has exactly $12$ common flex lines. This answers a question of Ciliberto, Miranda, and Roé. The main result of this paper was obtained using generative AI, particularly ChatGPT 5.5 Pro and the Danus system.

</details>


### [158] [An atomic criterion for irrationality without quantum computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26718)
*Vladimiro Benedetti,Aideen Fay,Jérémy Guéré,Laurent Manivel,Nicolas Perrin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需直接计算原子（atoms）即可推导非常一般成员非有理性的新判据，仅依赖于量子乘法的单值等变性和单值表示的不可约性。该判据适用于三次四重簇、Gushel-Mukai四重簇以及Küchle (c5)型四重簇（一种指数为1的Fano流形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Katzarkov-Kontsevich-Pantev-Yu引入的双有理不变量需要利用量子上同调良好的簇来获得非有理性结果，但原子计算通常较为复杂。本文希望找到一种更简便的判据，避免直接计算原子，仅通过量子乘法的单值等变性和单值表示的不可约性来判定非有理性。

Method: 在某些上同调条件下，利用量子乘法的单值等变性与单值表示的不可约性，建立无需计算原子即可推导非有理性的判据。

Result: 该判据成功应用于非常一般的三次四重簇和Gushel-Mukai四重簇（已知非有理性），并首次证明非常一般的Küchle (c5)型四重簇（指数为1的Fano流形）的非有理性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的简便判据，用于判定某些簇的非有理性，扩展了这类方法的应用范围。

Abstract: The birational invariants introduced by Katzarkov-Kontsevich-Pantev-Yu allows one to obtain irrationality results for varieties whose quantum cohomology is well-behaved. We observe that under certain cohomological conditions, we can deduce irrationality of a very general member from the theory of atoms without actually computing them, using only monodromy equivariance of quantum multiplication and irreducibility of the monodromy representation. Our criterion applies to the very general cubic and Gushel-Mukai fourfolds, whose irrationalities were already known, but also to the very general K{ü}chle fourfold of type (c5), which is a Fano manifold of index one.

</details>


### [159] [Image Recognition via Vaisman--Neifeld's Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26749)
*N. C. Combe,H. K. Nencka*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合Vaisman和Neifeld框架的几何积分与拓扑分析方法，通过将配置空间分解为不变叶理和矩映射，解决了欠定逆问题中的内在歧义，建立了抗噪且计算高效的统一重建框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在从非完整数据重建隐藏结构时，难以处理欠定逆问题中的内在歧义，缺乏统一的几何与拓扑视角来指导重建过程。

Method: 将配置空间精细分解为不变叶理和矩映射，融合Vaisman的对称性洞察与Neifeld的分析方法，构建抗噪且计算可行的统一框架。

Result: 建立了鲁棒、抗噪的重建框架，确保计算可行性，并为成像和结构分析提供了统一视角。

Conclusion: 该框架能应用于多科学领域，凸显了几何与拓扑在求解逆问题中的协同作用。

Abstract: We introduce a new approach to the reconstruction of hidden structures from incomplete data, unifying techniques from geometric integration and topological analysis within the frameworks of Vaisman and Neifeld. Our method employs a refined geometric decomposition of configuration spaces into invariant foliations and moment maps, thereby addressing the intrinsic ambiguities of underdetermined inverse problems. By combining Vaisman's insights into symmetry with Neifeld's analytical methodologies, we establish a robust, noise-resistant framework that ensures computational tractability while providing a unified perspective on reconstruction in imaging and structural analysis. This approach enables applications across diverse scientific domains and highlights the interplay between geometry and topology in the solution of inverse problems.

</details>


### [160] [On the Betti numbers, Poincaré polynomials, and Euler characteristics of $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26755)
*Giordano Cotti*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了稳定n点有理曲线Deligne-Mumford模空间\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}的Poincaré多项式、Betti数和欧拉示性数。给出了Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento和Eur-Ferroni-Matherne-Pagaria-Vecchi两个近期闭公式的初等推导，表明它们已隐含在Getzler和Manin的生成级数结果中。通过生成函数、二项式级数和Stirling数恒等式等初等操作提取。还发现了新的线性递推关系，得到了两个新Betti数公式。利用Lambert W函数的泰勒展开，展示了欧拉示性数序列可通过在显式辅助整数序列上求值完全Bell多项式得到，由此导出Hessenberg行列式公式和新线性递推，并给出了欧拉示性数的完整渐近展开，细化了Manin-Zagier的渐近估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}的Poincaré多项式和欧拉示性数提供更初等、统一的推导方法，揭示已有公式之间的内在联系，并发现新的递推关系和渐近展开。

Method: 使用生成函数的初等操作、二项式级数展开、Stirling数恒等式、Lambert W函数的泰勒展开以及完全Bell多项式的求值。

Result: 给出了两个已有闭公式的初等推导，发现了新的线性递推关系，得到了两个未曾在文献中记录的新Betti数公式，导出了欧拉示性数的Hessenberg行列式公式和新线性递推，给出了完整的渐近展开。

Conclusion: Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento和Eur-Ferroni-Matherne-Pagaria-Vecchi的公式已隐含在Getzler-Manin的结果中，可通过初等方法提取；欧拉示性数可通过Bell多项式与Lambert W函数建立新的联系，其渐近行为可被完整刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the Poincaré polynomials, Betti numbers, and Euler characteristics of the Deligne-Mumford moduli spaces $\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n}$ of stable $n$-pointed rational curves. We give elementary derivations of two recent closed formulas for their Poincaré polynomials, due respectively to Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento (arXiv:2406.13095) and to Eur-Ferroni-Matherne-Pagaria-Vecchi (arXiv:2504.16776). Our approach shows that both formulas are already implicit in the generating-series results of Getzler and Manin, and can be extracted from them by elementary manipulations of generating functions, the binomial series, and standard identities for Stirling numbers. Beyond these new derivations, the same method also yields new linear recurrence relations for refined invariants associated with these Poincaré polynomials, namely distinguished summands and a bivariate refinement. As a further consequence, we obtain two additional formulas for the Betti numbers, not previously recorded in this form.
  We also study the Euler characteristics $χ(\overline{\mathcal M}_{0,n})$. Using the Taylor expansion of a suitable branch of the Lambert $W$-function, we show that their sequence is obtained by evaluating complete Bell polynomials at an explicit auxiliary integer sequence. This Bell-polynomial representation yields Hessenberg determinantal formulas and a new linear recursion, distinct from the well-known quadratic Keel-Manin recursion. It also provides an explicit extraction of the Euler characteristics from the Lambert $W$-function expression considered by Aluffi-Marcolli-Nascimento. Finally, we refine the Manin-Zagier asymptotic estimate for these Euler characteristics by computing the full asymptotic expansion.

</details>


### [161] [Fundamental group schemes of globally F-regular varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26785)
*Charles Vial*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了正特征代数闭域上连通正规全局F-正则射影簇的连通拟扭子覆盖塔必然稳定，且其正则区域的Nori基本群概形是有限且线性可约的。其中线性可约性表明所有拟扭子覆盖都是驯服的，而有限性则是Xu和Braun关于log Fano复射影簇光滑点基本群有限性定理的正特征类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征代数几何中，研究射影簇的基本群结构及其有限性是一个核心问题。受Xu和Braun在复几何中关于log Fano簇光滑点基本群有限性结果的启发，本文旨在将其推广到正特征情形，并探索全局F-正则性这一正特征奇点理论中的关键性质与基本群有限性之间的关系。

Method: 利用正特征代数几何中F-分裂与F-正则性理论，结合Nori基本群概形的框架，通过分析拟扭子覆盖塔的稳定性来建立基本群概形的有限性。同时，通过线性可约性论证所有拟扭子覆盖的驯服性。

Result: 建立了正特征代数闭域上连通正规全局F-正则射影簇的正则区域Nori基本群概形的有限性和线性可约性，并证明了其拟扭子覆盖塔的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该结果将Xu-Braun定理推广到了正特征情形，揭示了全局F-正则性与基本群有限性之间的深刻联系，为正特征代数几何中基本群理论的发展提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We prove that every tower of connected quasi-torsor covers of a connected normal globally $F$-regular projective variety over an algebraically closed field of positive characteristic stabilizes, and that the Nori fundamental group scheme of its regular locus is finite and linearly reductive. The linear reductivity asserts that any quasi-torsor cover is tame, while the finiteness is a positive-characteristic analogue of theorems of Xu and Braun on the finiteness of the fundamental group of the smooth locus of a log Fano complex projective variety.

</details>


### [162] [A non-holomorphic P=W phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26806)
*Zili Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维孤立簇变体在不满足满秩条件下的P=W恒等式。通过实解析几何构造了P侧的perverse截断，在W侧研究了常层沿自然非正常代数态射的导出推前的分解。发现当不满足满秩条件时，两侧的hard Lefschetz性质均失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将P=W恒等式推广到更一般的簇变体，特别是去除满秩假设这一强条件，并处理簇变体奇异且Lagrangian纤维化非复代数的情况。

Method: 在P侧通过精细的实解析几何分析构造perverse截断；在W侧构造非正常代数态射并研究混合Hodge模导出范畴中常层的导出推前分解。同时分析curious hard Lefschetz和relative hard Lefschetz性质。

Result: 成功证明了三维孤立簇变体在不满足满秩假设条件下的P=W恒等式，且发现了当满秩条件不成立时，W侧的curious hard Lefschetz性质和P侧的relative hard Lefschetz性质均会失效。

Conclusion: P=W恒等式在更广泛的簇变体上成立，但经典hard Lefschetz性质依赖于满秩条件，揭示了P=W现象与Lefschetz性质之间的微妙关系。

Abstract: We prove the P=W identity for isolated cluster varieties of dimension 3 without the full rank hypothesis. These cluster varieties are generally singular, and the associated Lagrangian fibrations are not complex algebraic. On the P side, we construct the perverse truncation by a detailed analysis of the explicit real-analytic geometry of the Lagrangian fibration. On the W side, we construct a natural non-proper algebraic morphism from the cluster varieties and investigate the decomposition of the derived push-forward of the constant sheaf along this morphism within the derived category of mixed Hodge modules. In the P=W phenomenon for non-full rank isolated cluster varieties of dimension 3, both the curious hard Lefschetz property on the W side and the relative hard Lefschetz property on the P side fail.

</details>


### [163] [Splitting Brauer classes by genus one curves over number fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26915)
*Benjamin Antieau,Asher Auel,Federico Scavia*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了数域上至少二维的Severi-Brauer簇包含扭曲椭圆正规曲线，进而每个Brauer类可被亏格一曲线分裂。通过纤维化方法，证明了扭曲椭圆正规曲线的Hilbert scheme的光滑紧化的有理点在其Brauer-Manin集中稠密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数域上Severi-Brauer簇中扭曲椭圆正规曲线的存在性，以及Brauer类被亏格一曲线分裂的问题。

Method: 采用纤维化方法，通过证明扭曲椭圆正规曲线的Hilbert scheme的光滑紧化的有理点在其Brauer-Manin集中稠密来推导主要结论。

Result: 证明了数域上任意至少二维的Severi-Brauer簇包含扭曲椭圆正规曲线，且每个Brauer类可被亏格一曲线分裂。

Conclusion: 数域上至少二维的Severi-Brauer簇都包含扭曲椭圆正规曲线，从而Brauer类都被亏格一曲线分裂。

Abstract: We prove that every Severi-Brauer variety of dimension at least two over a number field contains a twisted elliptic normal curve. Consequently, every Brauer class over a number field is split by a genus one curve. We prove this using the fibration method, by showing that the rational points on a smooth compactification of the Hilbert scheme of twisted elliptic normal curves are dense in its Brauer-Manin set.

</details>


### [164] [Removing the Torsion-free Hypothesis in a Positivity Theorem on Deligne-Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26989)
*Shengyu Hou,Jihao Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文移除了Casalaina-Martin和Zhjeqi的一个正性定理中的无挠假设，回答了他们的一个问题。该结果是通过生成式AI（ChatGPT 5.5 Pro和Danus系统）获得的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Casalaina-Martin和Zhjeqi提出的关于移除其正性定理中无挠假设的问题。

Method: 使用生成式AI工具（ChatGPT 5.5 Pro和Danus系统）进行推理和证明。

Result: 成功移除了原定理中的无挠假设，推广了该正性定理。

Conclusion: 无挠假设在该正性定理中并非必要，且生成式AI可辅助数学研究。

Abstract: We remove the torsion-free hypothesis from a positivity theorem of Casalaina-Martin and Zhjeqi, answering a question of them. The main result of this paper was obtained using generative AI, particularly ChatGPT 5.5 Pro and the Danus system.

</details>


### [165] [The finite degree formula for normalized volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27032)
*Zhiyu Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于klt奇点之间有限满射态射的归一化体积公式，即$\widehat{\mathrm{vol}}(x, X, Δ_X)=\mathrm{deg}(f)\cdot \widehat{\mathrm{vol}}(y, Y, Δ_Y)$，从而验证了[LLX20, Zhu25, XZ26a]中的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证[LLX20, Zhu25, XZ26a]中提出的关于klt奇点在有限满射态射下归一化体积行为的猜想，该猜想是奇点理论中关于体积不变量研究的重要问题。

Method: 利用klt奇点的结构性质以及有限满射态射$f: (X, Δ_X) \to (Y, Δ_Y)$满足$K_X+Δ_X=f^*(K_Y+Δ_Y)$的条件，通过代数几何中的体积理论推导得出归一化体积的等式关系。

Result: 建立了$\widehat{\mathrm{vol}}(x, X, Δ_X)=\mathrm{deg}(f)\cdot \widehat{\mathrm{vol}}(y, Y, Δ_Y)$这一精确等式，将态射的度数直接与归一化体积的缩放因子联系起来。

Conclusion: 成功证明了该猜想，揭示了klt奇点在有限满射态射下归一化体积的倍乘行为，为奇点体积理论提供了新的结构结果。

Abstract: Let $f\colon \big(x\in (X, Δ_X)\big)\to \big(y\in (Y, Δ_Y)\big)$ be a finite surjective morphism between klt singularities such that $K_X+Δ_X=f^*(K_Y+Δ_Y)$. We show that the normalized volumes satisfy \[\widehat{\mathrm{vol}}(x, X, Δ_X)=\mathrm{deg}(f)\cdot \widehat{\mathrm{vol}}(y, Y, Δ_Y).\] This proves a conjecture in [LLX20, Zhu25, XZ26a].

</details>


### [166] [On the boundedness of elliptic Calabi-Yau 4-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27048)
*Stefano Filipazzi,Fulin Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了椭圆Calabi-Yau 4-fold的有界性：除非是Y×E的商（其中E是椭圆曲线，Y是CY 3-fold），否则这类簇属于有限代数族。特别地，非等平凡椭圆纤维化的CY 4-fold满足该性质。此外还提供了CY 3-fold中间Betti数有界性的部分证据，并研究了纤维化K-平凡4-fold的指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究椭圆Calabi-Yau 4-fold的有界性问题，这是双有理几何和模空间理论中的重要课题。理解这类簇的分类和有限性对弦理论和代数几何均有重要意义。

Method: 利用双有理几何和纤维化结构分析，通过区分是否可化为Y×E的商来划分情况；结合代数族的概念和Betti数有界性分析，研究纤维化K-平凡4-fold的指数。

Result: 证明了大部分椭圆Calabi-Yau 4-fold（非等于Y×E之商者）属于有限代数族；非等平凡纤维化情形自动满足该结论；给出了CY 3-fold中间Betti数有界性的部分结果。

Conclusion: 椭圆Calabi-Yau 4-fold在排除特殊退化情形后具有有界性，这推进了Calabi-Yau簇的分类理论。

Abstract: In this work, we settle the boundedness of a vast class of elliptic Calabi-Yau 4-folds. In particular, we show that any elliptic Calabi-Yau 4-fold that is not crepant to the quotient of a product $Y \times E$, where $E$ is an elliptic curve and $Y$ a Calabi-Yau 3-fold, belongs to finitely many algebraic families. In particular, the statement applies whenever the elliptic fibration of the Calabi-Yau 4-fold is not isotrivial. We also provide partial evidence for the boundedness of the middle Betti number of Calabi-Yau 3-folds and study the index of fibered $K$-trivial 4-folds.

</details>

### [167] [Quantum Turing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26331)
*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Lindblad格点动力学中构造了量子图灵斑图，建立了关于非线性序和量子涨落的严格理论。通过完全正定有限程耦合系统，证明了第一矩方程在非零波数处的超临界不稳定性，得到了解析的格心和键心共度条带分支。在半经典极限下，投影相干态在每个有界时间区间上表现出广泛Bragg序。证明了微观协方差以O(N^{-1/2})收敛到非自治高斯Lyapunov流，将严格的偏转置不确定性违背传递到足够大的N。在均匀高斯扇区中，单个无量纲比同时控制图灵稳定性行列式和相反动量的对数负性，将波长选择直接关联到量子纠缠。微分输运将最强的相反动量相关性从红外转移到选定的图灵尺度。数值延拓和二维模拟展示了条带、斑点和迷宫形态，其傅里叶模和涨落谱集中在相同的选定波数上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典图灵斑图理论扩展到量子体系，在开放量子系统的Lindblad动力学框架下建立量子图灵斑图的严格数学理论，并揭示量子纠缠与斑图波长选择之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用Lindblad主方程描述开放量子格点系统，通过第一矩方程分析超临界不稳定性，利用半经典极限和投影相干态研究量子涨落，通过Lyapunov流理论分析协方差收敛性，结合数值延拓和二维模拟验证理论预测。

Result: (1) 证明了第一矩方程在非零波数处存在超临界不稳定性，并解析得到了稳定的格心和键心共度条带分支；(2) 建立了微观协方差到非自治高斯Lyapunov流的O(N^{-1/2})收敛性；(3) 发现单个无量纲比同时控制图灵稳定性行列式和相反动量的对数负性；(4) 微分输运将最强相反动量相关性从红外转移到选定图灵尺度；(5) 数值模拟展示了条带、斑点和迷宫形态，且傅里叶模和涨落谱集中在相同选定波数。

Conclusion: 成功建立了量子图灵斑图的严格理论框架，揭示了量子纠缠与斑图波长选择的直接关联，为理解量子系统中的自发对称性破缺和斑图形成提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We construct quantum Turing patterns in Lindblad lattice dynamics and establish a rigorous theory of their nonlinear order and quantum fluctuations. For an explicit completely positive family with finite-range couplings, the first-moment equations undergo a supercritical instability at a nonzero wave number and admit analytic site- and bond-centered commensurate stripe branches. These branches are locally asymptotically stable in their reflection-fixed period-cell spaces, and projected coherent states exhibit extensive Bragg order on every bounded time interval in the semiclassical limit. We prove $O(N^{-1/2})$ convergence of microscopic covariances to a nonautonomous Gaussian Lyapunov flow, transferring strict partial-transpose uncertainty violations to sufficiently large $N$. In the homogeneous Gaussian sector, a single dimensionless ratio controls both the Turing stability determinant and the logarithmic negativity of opposite momenta, relating wavelength selection directly to quantum entanglement. Differential transport shifts the strongest opposite-momentum correlations from the infrared to the selected Turing scale. Numerical continuation and two-dimensional simulations display stripe, spot, and labyrinth morphologies whose Fourier modes and fluctuation spectra concentrate at the same selected wave numbers.

</details>


### [168] [The boundary-driven multispecies harmonic process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26262)
*Francesco Casini,Rouven Frassek,Cristian Giardinà*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了多物种版本的谐和过程，即一维链上与边界储库接触的连续时间马尔可夫链。通过将马尔可夫生成元与开放有理海森堡自旋链的哈密顿量对应，推导了R-和K-矩阵，并构造了双行转移矩阵。R-矩阵可分解为两个分别对应左移和右移粒子的R-算符。此外还定义了三种对偶模型：吸收粒子模型、隐藏参数模型和热传导模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单物种谐和过程推广到多物种情形，研究非平衡态下多色粒子的输运行为，并利用可积系统理论建立严格的数学框架。

Method: 利用可积系统理论，将多物种谐和过程的马尔可夫生成元映射为开放有理海森堡自旋链的哈密顿量，通过Sklyanin方法推导R-和K-矩阵并构造双行转移矩阵。

Result: 成功建立了多物种谐和过程与可积自旋链的对应关系，推导了算符形式的R-和K-矩阵，并发现R-矩阵可分解为左/右移动粒子的两个R-算符。还定义了三种对偶模型。

Conclusion: 多物种谐和过程具有可积结构，丰富了非平衡统计物理中多物种输运模型的理论框架，为后续研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce the multispecies version of the harmonic process on a one-dimensional chain in contact with boundary reservoirs. This process is a continuous-time Markov chain where each site can host an unbounded number of colored particles. The symmetric bulk dynamics is put in contact with reservoirs, which inject and remove particles driving the system out-of-equilibrium. The Markov generator of the process is identified with the integrable Hamiltonian of an open rational Heisenberg spin chain of higher rank. We derive the underlying R- and K-matrices in operator form and construct the double-row transfer matrix following Sklyanin. Similar to the monospecies case the R-matrix factorises into two R-operators, each factor corresponding to left and right moving particles. We further define three dual models: an absorbing particle model, a hidden parameter model and a heat conduction model.

</details>


### [169] [Quantum estimates for classical polynomial optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25445)
*Oleg Evnin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受量子力学变分法启发的全新策略，用于寻找多元齐次多项式的下界和上界。该方法将原多项式替换为在大状态空间中作用的算子，通过对角化该量子算子（表示为大型矩阵）来近似系数张量的最小和最大特征值，从而获得多项式的界限。该方法成功应用于张量特征值文献中的标准测试示例及数学物理中的Strichartz型不等式等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找多元齐次多项式的上下界在动力系统稳定性、数据分析等领域具有重要意义，但传统基于张量特征值理论的非线性迭代方法因高阶张量多项式目标函数的复杂地形导致收敛不稳定，亟需一种更可靠的替代策略。

Method: 受量子力学变分法启发，将原多项式替换为在大状态空间中作用的算子，使得在适当的"经典"极限下该算子趋近于由交换变量构成的原始多项式表达式。通过对角化该量子算子（表示为大型矩阵），提取其最小和最大特征值，从而近似系数张量的最小和最大特征值，最终得到多项式的界限。

Result: 该方法成功应用于张量特征值文献中的标准测试示例，以及数学物理中具有重要意义的Strichartz型不等式等问题，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 量子变分方法为寻找多元齐次多项式界限提供了一种全新的、可靠的替代方案，避免了传统非线性迭代方法在张量情形下的收敛不稳定问题，并具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: The problem of finding lower and upper bounds on multivariate homogeneous polynomials is both difficult and important given its applications to questions ranging from dynamical stability in complex potential landscapes to data analysis. From the standpoint of tensor eigenvalue theory, the question is equivalent to finding the smallest and the largest eigenvalues of the coefficient tensor corresponding to the given polynomial. Standard approaches outlined in the literature amount to running nonlinear iterations in search for the optimal rays along which the growth of the polynomial is fastest or slowest. Unlike the case of matrices (or their corresponding multivariate quadratic forms) convergence of such algorithms for higher-rank tensors is capricious due to the complex topography of polynomial objective functions. In this essay, a very different strategy, inspired by quantum-mechanical variational methods, is introduced for finding bounds on polynomials. The original polynomial is replaced by an operator acting in a suitably chosen (large) space of states, such that in an appropriate "classical" limit this operator approaches the original polynomial expression made of commutative variables. As a result, approximating the smallest and largest eigenvalues of the coefficient tensor, and thus finding bounds on polynomials, amounts to diagonalizing the resulting quantum operator, represented as a large matrix, and then inspecting the smallest and largest eigenvalues of this matrix. This approach is then successfully applied to standard test examples from tensor eigenvalue literature and other problems of interest in mathematical physics including Strichartz-type inequalities.

</details>


### [170] [Towards First Quantisation Formalism for AKSZ Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26394)
*Leon Menger,Pavel Mnev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将AKSZ理论T通过图上的1维AKSZ理论t进行“第一量子化”的方法，使得t的配分函数重现T的费曼图。该理论t将边长度T及其微分dT解释为引力子和引力微子的零模，并在BV-BFV形式下研究。李代数为su(2)的非阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论被作为示例，描述了顶点拉格朗日子流形L_W（Wigner拉格朗日量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上AKSZ理论（如Chern-Simons、泊松sigma模型等）的量子化通过费曼图展开来定义，但缺乏一个统一的几何框架来理解这些费曼图的结构。本文旨在构建一个1维图上的AKSZ理论t，使得费曼图的生成成为一个自洽的量子场论过程，从而将图上的组合结构与目标流形上的几何结构联系起来。

Method: 1. 在BV-BFV形式化框架下，构造1维AKSZ理论t，其目标空间包含底流形M，并耦合1维超引力。2. 将度规图的边长度T及其de Rham微分dT分别解释为引力子和引力微子的零模。3. 在经典层面，将顶点分配到相空间Φ的Cartesian幂中的拉格朗日子流形L_k，这些子流形定义Weinstein辛范畴中的循环L_∞-代数。4. 通过路径积分构造，拉格朗日量L_k作为k价顶点上入射边的缝合条件。

Result: 成功构建了1维AKSZ理论t，其配分函数积分产生T的费曼图之和。t在BV-BFV框架下的规范固定与T的规范固定相对应。证明了Witten-Morse超对称量子力学可作为特定的规范固定示例。对于su(2)非阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论，给出了顶点拉格朗日量L_W的具体描述。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了AKSZ理论与图上的1维场论之间的严格对应关系，将费曼图展开几何化为一个超引力耦合的1维AKSZ理论的路径积分。这为理解量子场论的费曼图结构提供了新的视角，并为后续研究如更高维度的推广、与拓扑递归的关系等奠定了基础。

Abstract: \noindent Given an AKSZ theory $\mathbb{T}$ on a manifold $M$, with target a graded vector space $Y$, we formulate a 1-dimensional theory $\mathbb{t}$ on graphs (the ``first quantisation picture for $\mathbb{T}$''), whose partition functions reproduce the Feynman graphs of $\mathbb{T}$. More precisely, the theory $\mathbb{t}$ is itself a 1d AKSZ theory with the target built out of $M$, and involving a coupling to 1d supergravity. It yields a form on the space of metric graphs (with length $T$ of an edge and its de Rham differential $\mathrm{d} T$ interpreted as the zero-modes of the graviton and gravitino, respectively); its integral yields the sum of Feynman graphs of $\mathbb{T}$. We study the theory $\mathbb{t}$ in the BV-BFV formalism; a gauge-fixing of $\mathbb{T}$ corresponds to a gauge-fixing of $\mathbb{t}$. At the classical level, $\mathbb{t}$ assigns to vertices certain Lagrangian submanifolds $L_k$ in Cartesian powers $Φ^{\times k}$ of the phase space $Φ$ of $\mathbb{t}$. These submanifolds can be thought of as defining a cyclic $\mathrm{L}_\infty$-algebra in Weinstein's symplectic category (``dequantising'' the cohomological vector field on the target %target AKSZ dg structure of $\mathbb{T}$). In the path integral construction of $\mathbb{t}$, Lagrangians $L_k$ determine sewing conditions for fields on the incident edges at a $k$-valent vertex. We give examples of this paradigm, such as when $\mathbb{t}$ on edges is the Witten-Morse supersymmetric quantum mechanics (which corresponds to a particular type of gauge-fixing for $\mathbb{T}$ and $\mathbb{t}$). In the example where $\mathbb{T}$ is the non-abelian Chern--Simons theory with structure Lie algebra $\mathfrak{su}(2)$, we describe the vertex Lagrangian $L_{\mathrm{W}}$ (the ``Wigner Lagrangian'' ).

</details>


### [171] [From Quantum-Plane Hamiltonians to Jackson Dynamics: Dilation Representations, Normal Symbols, and Euclidean Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26454)
*Xiaomei Yang,Zhiliang Deng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子平面上形式q-哈密顿力学（基于非对易坐标和协变q-导数）与可计算实现（基于Jackson有限差分的常微分系统）之间的严格桥梁，通过表示-符号对应、正规排序和星积框架，证明了形式q-导数与Jackson算子的精确交织关系，并分析了q→1极限下向经典哈密顿力学的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子平面上的形式q-哈密顿力学使用非对易坐标和协变q-导数表述，但其实际计算通常依赖于涉及Jackson有限差分的常微分系统。这两个层次之间的严格数学对应关系尚未被清晰建立，这限制了q-变形哈密顿蒙特卡洛等方法的理论基础。

Method: 通过构造表示-符号对应：将量子平面坐标代数及其协变微分运算实现为光滑交换函数空间上的乘法、膨胀和Jackson算子；利用正规排序将坐标代数与多项式符号空间等同，并赋予显式的结合星积；在此框架下证明形式q-导数与Jackson算子的精确交织，以及形式q-哈密顿作用到符号上的精确星-Jackson作用。

Result: 对于坐标可观测量，星积修正消失，精确恢复可计算的Jackson坐标方程；对于一般可观测量，用普通乘法替代星积产生受控的一阶误差；哈密顿符号的乘法算子是所表示的算子哈密顿量的领先交换近似。建立了Euclidean Jackson向量场，并证明了q→1极限下算子、符号、向量场和有限时间轨迹到经典哈密顿动力学的一阶收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文为量子平面形式哈密顿力学与q-变形哈密顿蒙特卡洛中使用的实际动力学提供了严格的理论连接，验证了可计算近似的精确性和极限行为。

Abstract: Formal $q$-Hamiltonian mechanics on the quantum plane is expressed through noncommuting coordinates and covariant $q$-derivatives, whereas its computable realization is usually written as an ordinary differential system involving Jackson finite differences. In this paper, we formulate the passage between these two levels through a representation--symbol correspondence. The quantum-plane coordinate algebra and its covariant differential calculus are realized by multiplication, dilation, and Jackson operators on a smooth commutative function space. Normal ordering then identifies the coordinate algebra with a polynomial symbol space endowed with an explicit associative star product. Within this framework, the formal $q$-derivatives intertwine exactly with the corresponding Jackson operators, and the formal $q$-Hamiltonian action descends to an exact star-Jackson action on symbols. For the coordinate observables, the star-product corrections vanish, so the computable Jackson coordinate equations are recovered exactly. For general observables, replacing the star product by ordinary multiplication produces a controlled first-order error. We also show that multiplication by the Hamiltonian symbol is the leading commutative approximation of the represented operator Hamiltonian. Finally, we study the resulting Euclidean Jackson vector field and establish first-order convergence of the operator, symbol, vector-field, and finite-time trajectory formulations to classical Hamiltonian dynamics as $q\to1$. These results provide a rigorous bridge between formal quantum-plane Hamiltonian mechanics and the dynamics used in $q$-deformed Hamiltonian Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [172] [Congruence of Dirac operators with applications to generalized MIT bag models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26675)
*Joaquim Duran,Konstantin Pankrashkin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单的同余变换方法，用于改变具有壳层相互作用的狄拉克算子的耦合参数，并由此得到了关于广义MIT袋模型的自伴性及无限质量解释的新发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过引入同余变换来调整狄拉克算子中的耦合参数，从而解决广义MIT袋模型中的自伴性问题并澄清其无限质量解释。

Method: 采用同余变换（congruence transform）技术，系统地改变具有壳层相互作用的狄拉克算子的耦合参数，并对变换后的算子性质进行分析。

Result: 成功实现了耦合参数的移位，并基于该变换获得了关于广义MIT袋模型自伴性的新观察结果，同时深化了对无限质量解释的理解。

Conclusion: 该同余变换为研究壳层相互作用的狄拉克算子提供了有效工具，并为广义MIT袋模型的理论基础提供了新的见解。

Abstract: We highlight a simple congruence transform that shifts coupling parameters for Dirac operators with shell interactions. As one of the consequences, this leads to new observations concerning the self-adjointness and the infinite mass interpretation of generalized MIT bag models.

</details>


### [173] [Lee-Yang Zeros And Particle Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26975)
*Mohamed El Hedi Bahri,Ian Jauslin,Joel L. Lebowitz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在连续系统中，若李-杨零点在复逸度平面上远离正实数点z₀，则巨正则系综的热力学极限与求导运算可交换，有限体积压强的所有导数一致收敛到极限压强的对应导数，且密度和粒子数方差分别收敛到β⁻¹∂_μ p和β⁻²∂_μ² p。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计力学中，相变与巨配分函数零点（李-杨零点）的行为密切相关。本文旨在建立李-杨零点远离正实轴这一条件与热力学极限下物理量（如密度、方差）的可微性之间的严格数学联系，即证明零点无碍区域中极限与求导的可交换性。

Method: 在巨正则系综框架下，考虑连续经典粒子系统，假设对势满足稳定性、缓减性和低正则性，边界条件具有一致有界密度。利用李-杨零点在复逸度平面上有界远离z₀这一前提，沿立方体序列证明有限体积压强的导数在z₀邻域一致收敛到极限压强的导数，并推至密度与方差收敛。

Result: (1) 若李-杨零点在所有足够大体积下保持远离正实数点z₀，则热力学极限与对化学势的求导在z₀处可交换；(2) 所有导数的极限值独立于边界条件；(3) 密度收敛到β⁻¹∂_μ p，粒子数方差收敛到β⁻²∂_μ² p；(4) 结果可推广至Procacci-Yuhjtman无界边界条件及Ruelle缓边界条件。

Conclusion: 李-杨零点远离正实轴不仅是相变缺失的充分条件，也是导数型热力学量（密度、压缩率等）在热力学极限下良好定义并独立于边界条件的充分条件。该结果为经典统计力学中零点分析方法提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: We consider classical particles in the continuum in the grand canonical ensemble, with a stable, tempered and lower-regular pair potential and boundary conditions of uniformly bounded density. We prove that if the Lee--Yang zeros of the grand canonical partition function in the complex fugacity plane $z = e^{βμ}$ remain bounded away from a real point $z_0 > 0$ for all sufficiently large volumes, then along cubes the thermodynamic limit and differentiation commute at $z_0$: every derivative of the finite-volume pressure in the chemical potential converges, uniformly in a neighborhood of $z_0$, to the corresponding derivative of the limiting pressure. The limiting values of all derivatives are independent of the boundary condition; in particular, the density and the particle-number variance per unit volume converge to $β^{-1}\partial_μp$ and $β^{-2}\partial^{2}_μ p$, respectively. The result extends to the unbounded boundary conditions of Procacci and Yuhjtman for super-stable potentials in addition to Ruelle's tempered boundary conditions.

</details>
